Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me create a comprehensive bug report with the analysis.

## Bug Analysis Report: OpenCode Session Resumption Not Creating Checkpoints

Based on my thorough code investigation, I've identified the root cause and the complete flow of the bug. Here's my detailed analysis:

### **The Problem**

When a user resumes an OpenCode session with `opencode -s <session-id>` that hasn't been committed yet:
1. ✅ First session: Creates checkpoint and suggests trailer
2. ❌ Resumed session: No new checkpoint is created, no trailer suggestion on next commit

### **Root Cause Analysis**

The bug is in **`InitializeSession` logic for resumed sessions** in `/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli_experiments/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` (lines 1397-1461).

**Key code section (lines 1409-1461):**
```go
if state != nil && state.BaseCommit != "" {
    // Session is fully initialized — apply phase transition for TurnStart.
    // ... [handle resumed session] ...
    return nil  // <-- EARLY RETURN!
}
// Initialize new session
state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, transcriptPath, userPrompt)
```

**The Problem:** When a session is resumed (state exists with BaseCommit set), the code returns early at line 1460 WITHOUT calling `initializeSession()`. This means:

1. **No new shadow branch is created** - The shadow branch was created in the FIRST session
2. **No new session state is properly initialized** - The resumed session reuses the old state
3. **On TurnEnd, SaveStep fails silently** - When `SaveStep` is called (line 24 of `manual_commit_git.go`):
   - It tries to load the session state (line 34)
   - It calls `ShadowBranchExists()` (line 60) which returns `true` (the old shadow branch from the first session)
   - But the checkpoint logic in `WriteTemporary` may skip or not properly handle resumed sessions

### **Detailed Flow Comparison**

**Fresh Session (WORKS ✅):**
1. `SessionStart` hook → `handleLifecycleSessionStart()` (lifecycle.go:61)
2. `TurnStart` hook → `handleLifecycleTurnStart()` → `InitializeSession()`
3. In `InitializeSession()` → Goes to line 1466 → Calls `s.initializeSession()` → Creates session state with `BaseCommit = HEAD`
4. `TurnEnd` hook → `SaveStep()` → `WriteTemporary()` → **Creates shadow branch** because `branchExisted = false` (line 60 of manual_commit_git.go)
5. User commits → Trailer is suggested

**Resumed Session (BROKEN ❌):**
1. `SessionStart` hook → Shows banner ✅
2. `TurnStart` hook → Calls `InitializeSession()`
3. In `InitializeSession()` → Session state already exists with `BaseCommit != ""` → **Returns early at line 1460** (before `initializeSession()` is called)
4. State is partially updated (new TurnID, updated transcript path, cleared checkpoint IDs) but **shadow branch is not reset**
5. `TurnEnd` hook → `SaveStep()` → `WriteTemporary()` → `branchExisted = true` (line 60)
6. **No new checkpoint is created on the shadow branch** OR checkpoint is created but not linked to current session state properly
7. User commits → **No trailer suggestion** because no checkpoint was properly saved

### **Why the Checkpoint Doesn't Get Saved on Resume**

Looking at the code flow more carefully:

1. **First session:** Shadow branch `entire/<hash7>-<worktreeHash6>` is created with checkpoint
2. **Resume session:** Same `BaseCommit` (hasn't changed), same shadow branch name
3. **SaveStep on resume:**
   - Calls `WriteTemporary()` with `IsFirstCheckpoint = false` (line 84 of manual_commit_git.go)
   - Line 100 of temporary.go checks if tree hash matches last checkpoint → **May skip if no changes**
   - OR if tree changes are detected, checkpoint IS created but without the session state being properly synchronized

**The Critical Missing Link:** When a session resumes after the FIRST checkpoint was created but BEFORE user commits:
- The session state file still exists in `.git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json`
- The shadow branch `entire/<hash>-<worktree>` already exists from the first session
- On resume, `InitializeSession()` detects the session exists and returns early
- This means the session's **turn counter and internal state are NOT reset for the new turn**
- The `SaveStep()` logic may:
  - Skip the checkpoint due to deduplication (tree hasn't changed)
  - Or create a checkpoint that isn't properly linked in the session state

### **The Fix Needed**

The `InitializeSession()` method needs to ensure that on EVERY turn (fresh or resumed):
1. Clear turn-specific checkpoint tracking (`LastCheckpointID`, `TurnCheckpointIDs`)
2. Reset the turn ID for correlation across multiple commits
3. Calculate attribution BEFORE the agent works
4. Save the updated state

Currently these steps ONLY happen for fresh sessions. For resumed sessions, step 3-4 happen (lines 1448-1449, 1457) but steps 1-2 happen (lines 1440-1441) WITHOUT the full context that comes from `initializeSession()`.

**Critical insight:** The code at lines 1437-1449 handles some of these concerns for resumed sessions, but the logic is incomplete because:
- It doesn't ensure the session state's internal correlations are fresh
- It doesn't properly rebuild the checkpoint tracking for a new turn
- The attribution calculation happens, but the session might be in an inconsistent state from the previous turn

### **Affected Code Paths**

1. **`/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli_experiments/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go:1397-1461`** - `InitializeSession()` early return for resumed sessions
2. **`/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli_experiments/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_git.go:24-150`** - `SaveStep()` relies on proper session state
3. **`/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli_experiments/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/temporary.go:56-150`** - `WriteTemporary()` deduplication logic may skip checkpoints on resume

### **Evidence from Code**

**Session resumption path (lines 1409-1460):**
```go
if state != nil && state.BaseCommit != "" {
    // Partial update for resumed session
    state.TurnID = turnID.String()                    // New turn ID
    state.LastCheckpointID = ""                       // Clear
    state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil                     // Clear
    promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(...) // Calculate
    state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
    // ... migrate shadow branch if needed ...
    s.saveSessionState(state)
    return nil  // <-- BUG: Returns without full initialization
}
```

Versus fresh session path (lines 1465-1485):
```go
state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, transcriptPath, userPrompt)
// This properly sets ALL fields including:
// - BaseCommit from HEAD
// - WorktreeID
// - UntrackedFilesAtStart
// - StartedAt
// - StepCount = 0
```

The resumed session path is missing the full context that fresh initialization provides.

### **Test Case That Should Fail (or IS failing)**

The test in `/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli_experiments/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/manual_commit_workflow_test.go` at line 1465 ("Continue the SAME session") should demonstrate this bug if running OpenCode specifically (though it may work with other agents due to different checkpoint handling).

---

**Summary:** The bug is that `InitializeSession()` for the manual-commit strategy doesn't fully reinitialize a resumed session - it returns early without ensuring the session state is in a clean state for checkpoint creation on the next turn.

  1496→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(

  1507→	var lastCheckpointTree *object.Tree
  1508→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1509→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)

    37→	}
    38→	// Initialize if state is nil OR BaseCommit is empty (can happen with partial state from warnings)
    39→	if state == nil || state.BaseCommit == "" {
    40→		agentType := resolveAgentType(ctx.AgentType, state)
    41→		state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, "", "") // No transcript/prompt in fallback
    42→		if err != nil {

    44→		}
    45→	}
    46→

    49→		return err
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	// Get checkpoint store

    54→	if err != nil {

    56→	}
    57→

    60→	branchExisted := store.ShadowBranchExists(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    61→
    62→	// Use the pending attribution calculated at prompt start (in InitializeSession)
    63→	// This was calculated BEFORE the agent made changes, so it accurately captures user edits
    64→	var promptAttr PromptAttribution
    65→	if state.PendingPromptAttribution != nil {

    99→	if err != nil {

    14→// Hook name constants — these become CLI subcommands under `entire hooks opencode`.
    15→const (
    16→	HookNameSessionStart = "session-start"
    17→	HookNameSessionEnd   = "session-end"
    18→	HookNameTurnStart    = "turn-start"
    19→	HookNameTurnEnd      = "turn-end"
    20→	HookNameCompaction   = "compaction"
    21→)
    22→
    23→// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    24→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) HookNames() []string {
    25→	return []string{
    26→		HookNameSessionStart,
    27→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    28→		HookNameTurnStart,
    29→		HookNameTurnEnd,
    30→		HookNameCompaction,
    31→	}
    32→}
    33→
    34→// ParseHookEvent translates OpenCode hook calls into normalized lifecycle events.
    35→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    36→	switch hookName {
    37→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    38→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    39→		if err != nil {
    40→			return nil, err
    41→		}
    42→		return &agent.Event{
    43→			Type:      agent.SessionStart,
    44→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
    45→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
    46→		}, nil
    47→
    48→	case HookNameTurnStart:
    49→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[turnStartRaw](stdin)
    50→		if err != nil {
    51→			return nil, err
    52→		}
    53→		// Get the temp file path for this session (may not exist yet, but needed for pre-prompt state).
    54→		// Repo root may fail if called outside a repo (unlikely in hook context).
    55→		// Fallback to "." is acceptable - the file will still be written, just
    56→		// to current directory instead of .entire/tmp/.
    57→		repoRoot, _ := paths.RepoRoot() //nolint:errcheck // see comment above
    58→		tmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)
    59→		transcriptPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, raw.SessionID+".json")
    60→		return &agent.Event{
    61→			Type:       agent.TurnStart,
    62→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
    63→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
    64→			Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
    65→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
    66→		}, nil
    67→
    68→	case HookNameTurnEnd:
    69→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    70→		if err != nil {
    71→			return nil, err
    72→		}
    73→		// Call `opencode export` to get the transcript and write to temp file
    74→		transcriptPath, exportErr := a.fetchAndCacheExport(raw.SessionID)
    75→		if exportErr != nil {
    76→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to export session: %w", exportErr)
    77→		}
    78→		return &agent.Event{
    79→			Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
    80→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
    81→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
    82→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
    83→		}, nil
    84→
    85→	case HookNameCompaction:
    86→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    87→		if err != nil {
    88→			return nil, err
    89→		}
    90→		return &agent.Event{
    91→			Type:      agent.Compaction,
    92→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
    93→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
    94→		}, nil
    95→
    96→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    97→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    98→		if err != nil {
    99→			return nil, err
   100→		}
   101→		return &agent.Event{
   102→			Type:      agent.SessionEnd,
   103→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
   104→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
   105→		}, nil
   106→
   107→	default:
   108→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action for unknown hooks
   109→	}
   110→}
   111→
   112→// fetchAndCacheExport calls `opencode export <sessionID>` and writes the result
   113→// to a temporary file. Returns the path to the temp file.
   114→//
   115→// Integration testing: Set ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT=1 to skip the
   116→// `opencode export` call and use pre-written mock data instead. Tests must
   117→// pre-write the transcript file to .entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json before
   118→// triggering the hook. See integration_test/hooks.go:SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd.
   119→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) fetchAndCacheExport(sessionID string) (string, error) {
   120→	// Get repo root for the temp directory
   121→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   122→	if err != nil {
   123→		repoRoot = "."
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	tmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)
   127→	tmpFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, sessionID+".json")
   128→
   129→	// Integration test mode: use pre-written mock file without calling opencode export
   130→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT") != "" {
   131→		if _, err := os.Stat(tmpFile); err == nil {
   132→			return tmpFile, nil
   133→		}
   134→		return "", fmt.Errorf("mock export file not found: %s (ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT is set)", tmpFile)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Call opencode export to get the transcript (always refresh on each turn)
   138→	data, err := runOpenCodeExport(sessionID)
   139→	if err != nil {
   140→		return "", fmt.Errorf("opencode export failed: %w", err)
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	// Write to temp directory under .entire
   144→	if err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   145→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create temp dir: %w", err)
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   149→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to write export file: %w", err)
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	return tmpFile, nil
   153→}
   154→

    15→
    16→const (
    17→	// pluginFileName is the name of the plugin file written to .opencode/plugins/
    18→	pluginFileName = "entire.ts"
    19→
    20→	// pluginDirName is the directory under .opencode/ where plugins live
    21→	pluginDirName = "plugins"
    22→
    23→	// entireMarker is a string present in the plugin file to identify it as Entire's
    24→	entireMarker = "Auto-generated by `entire enable --agent opencode`"
    25→)
    26→
    27→// getPluginPath returns the absolute path to the plugin file.
    28→func getPluginPath() (string, error) {
    29→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    30→	if err != nil {
    31→		// Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo (e.g., during tests)
    32→		//nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests run outside git repos)
    33→		repoRoot, err = os.Getwd()
    34→		if err != nil {
    35→			return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get current directory: %w", err)
    36→		}
    37→	}
    38→	return filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".opencode", pluginDirName, pluginFileName), nil
    39→}
    40→
    41→// InstallHooks writes the Entire plugin file to .opencode/plugins/entire.ts.
    42→// Returns 1 if the plugin was installed, 0 if already present (idempotent).
    43→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    44→	pluginPath, err := getPluginPath()
    45→	if err != nil {
    46→		return 0, err
    47→	}
    48→
    49→	// Check if already installed (idempotent) unless force
    50→	if !force {
    51→		if _, err := os.Stat(pluginPath); err == nil {
    52→			data, readErr := os.ReadFile(pluginPath) //nolint:gosec // Path constructed from repo root
    53→			if readErr == nil && strings.Contains(string(data), entireMarker) {
    54→				return 0, nil // Already installed
    55→			}
    56→		}
    57→	}
    58→
    59→	// Build the command prefix
    60→	var cmdPrefix string
    61→	if localDev {
    62→		cmdPrefix = "go run ${OPENCODE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go"
    63→	} else {
    64→		cmdPrefix = "entire"
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	// Generate plugin content from template
    68→	content := strings.ReplaceAll(pluginTemplate, entireCmdPlaceholder, cmdPrefix)
    69→
    70→	// Ensure directory exists
    71→	pluginDir := filepath.Dir(pluginPath)
    72→	//nolint:gosec // G301: Plugin directory needs standard permissions
    73→	if err := os.MkdirAll(pluginDir, 0o755); err != nil {
    74→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create plugin directory: %w", err)
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	// Write plugin file
    78→	//nolint:gosec // G306: Plugin file needs standard permissions for OpenCode to read
    79→	if err := os.WriteFile(pluginPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
    80→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write plugin file: %w", err)
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	return 1, nil
    84→}
    85→
    86→// UninstallHooks removes the Entire plugin file.
    87→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
    88→	pluginPath, err := getPluginPath()
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return err
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	if err := os.Remove(pluginPath); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
    94→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove plugin file: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	return nil
    98→}
    99→
   100→// AreHooksInstalled checks if the Entire plugin file exists and contains the marker.
   101→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   102→	pluginPath, err := getPluginPath()
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		return false
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	data, err := os.ReadFile(pluginPath) //nolint:gosec // Path constructed from repo root
   108→	if err != nil {
   109→		return false
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	return strings.Contains(string(data), entireMarker)
   113→}
   114→
   115→// GetSupportedHooks returns the normalized lifecycle events this agent supports.
   116→// OpenCode's native hooks map to standard agent lifecycle events:
   117→//   - session-start → HookSessionStart
   118→//   - session-end   → HookSessionEnd
   119→//   - turn-start    → HookUserPromptSubmit (user prompt triggers a turn)
   120→//   - turn-end      → HookStop (agent response complete)
   121→//
   122→// Note: HookNames() returns 5 hooks (including "compaction"), but GetSupportedHooks()
   123→// returns only 4. The "compaction" hook is OpenCode-specific with no standard HookType
   124→// mapping — it is handled via ParseHookEvent but not advertised as a standard lifecycle event.
   125→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   126→	return []agent.HookType{
   127→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   128→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   129→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   130→		agent.HookStop,
   131→	}
   132→}

    31→// hasTTY checks if /dev/tty is available for interactive prompts.
    32→// Returns false when running as an agent subprocess (no controlling terminal).
    33→//
    34→// In test environments, ENTIRE_TEST_TTY overrides the real check:
    35→//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 → simulate human (TTY available)
    36→//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0 → simulate agent (no TTY)
    37→func hasTTY() bool {
    38→	if v := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY"); v != "" {
    39→		return v == "1"
    40→	}
    41→
    42→	// Gemini CLI sets GEMINI_CLI=1 when running shell commands.
    43→	// Gemini subprocesses may have access to the user's TTY, but they can't
    44→	// actually respond to interactive prompts. Treat them as non-TTY.
    45→	// See: https://geminicli.com/docs/tools/shell/
    46→	if os.Getenv("GEMINI_CLI") != "" {
    47→		return false
    48→	}
    49→
    50→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		return false
    53→	}
    54→	_ = tty.Close()
    55→	return true
    56→}
    57→
    58→// askConfirmTTY prompts the user for a yes/no confirmation via /dev/tty.

    60→// Returns true for yes, false for no. If TTY is unavailable, returns the default.

    66→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") != "" {

    73→	if os.Getenv("GEMINI_CLI") != "" {

     1→// lifecycle.go implements the generic lifecycle event dispatcher.
     2→// It routes normalized events from any agent to the appropriate framework actions.
     3→//
     4→// The dispatcher inverts the current flow from "agent handler calls framework functions"
     5→// to "framework dispatcher calls agent methods." Agents are passive data providers;
     6→// the dispatcher handles all orchestration: state transitions, strategy calls,
     7→// file change detection, metadata generation.
     8→package cli
     9→
    10→import (
    11→	"context"
    12→	"errors"
    13→	"fmt"
    14→	"log/slog"
    15→	"os"
    16→	"path/filepath"
    17→	"strings"
    18→	"time"
    19→
    20→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    21→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    22→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    23→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    24→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    25→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    26→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/validation"
    27→)
    28→
    29→// DispatchLifecycleEvent routes a normalized lifecycle event to the appropriate handler.
    30→// Returns nil if the event was handled successfully.
    31→func DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    32→	if ag == nil {
    33→		return errors.New("agent cannot be nil")
    34→	}
    35→	if event == nil {
    36→		return errors.New("event cannot be nil")
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	switch event.Type {
    40→	case agent.SessionStart:
    41→		return handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag, event)
    42→	case agent.TurnStart:
    43→		return handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag, event)
    44→	case agent.TurnEnd:
    45→		return handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event)
    46→	case agent.Compaction:
    47→		return handleLifecycleCompaction(ag, event)
    48→	case agent.SessionEnd:
    49→		return handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag, event)
    50→	case agent.SubagentStart:
    51→		return handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag, event)
    52→	case agent.SubagentEnd:
    53→		return handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag, event)
    54→	default:
    55→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown lifecycle event type: %d", event.Type)
    56→	}
    57→}
    58→
    59→// handleLifecycleSessionStart handles session start: shows banner, checks concurrent sessions,
    60→// fires state machine transition.
    61→func handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    62→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
    63→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
    64→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
    65→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
    66→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
    67→	)
    68→
    69→	if event.SessionID == "" {
    70→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
    71→	}
    72→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(event.SessionID); err != nil {
    73→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	// Build informational message
    77→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
    78→
    79→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
    80→	strat := GetStrategy()
    81→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
    82→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(event.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
    83→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Output informational message
    88→	if event.ResponseMessage != "" {
    89→		message = event.ResponseMessage
    90→	}
    91→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
    92→		return err
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
    96→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
    97→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
    98→	} else if state != nil {
    99→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   100→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   101→		}
   102→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   103→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   104→		}
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	return nil
   108→}
   109→
   110→// handleLifecycleTurnStart handles turn start: captures pre-prompt state,
   111→// ensures strategy setup, initializes session.
   112→func handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   113→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   114→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-start",
   115→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   116→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   117→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   118→	)
   119→
   120→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   121→	if sessionID == "" {
   122→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
   123→	}
   124→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   125→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	// Capture pre-prompt state (including transcript position via TranscriptAnalyzer)
   129→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(ag, sessionID, event.SessionRef); err != nil {
   130→		return err
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	// Ensure strategy setup and initialize session
   134→	strat := GetStrategy()
   135→
   136→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   137→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   141→		agentType := ag.Type()
   142→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(sessionID, agentType, event.SessionRef, event.Prompt); err != nil {
   143→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   144→		}
   145→	}
   146→
   147→	return nil
   148→}
   149→
   150→// handleLifecycleTurnEnd handles turn end: validates transcript, extracts metadata,
   151→// detects file changes, saves step + checkpoint, transitions phase.
   152→//
   153→//nolint:maintidx // high complexity due to sequential orchestration of 8 steps (validation, extraction, file detection, filtering, token calc, step save, phase transition, cleanup) - splitting would obscure the flow
   154→func handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   155→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   156→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-end",
   157→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   158→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   159→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   160→	)
   161→
   162→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   163→	if sessionID == "" {
   164→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	transcriptRef := event.SessionRef
   168→	if transcriptRef == "" {
   169→		return errors.New("transcript file not specified")
   170→	}
   171→	if !fileExists(transcriptRef) {
   172→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found: %s", transcriptRef)
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   176→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   177→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   178→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Create session metadata directory
   182→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   183→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir
   186→	}
   187→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   188→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// If agent implements TranscriptPreparer, wait for transcript to be ready
   192→	if preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {
   193→		if err := preparer.PrepareTranscript(transcriptRef); err != nil {
   194→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to prepare transcript: %v\n", err)
   195→		}
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Copy transcript to session directory
   199→	transcriptData, err := ag.ReadTranscript(transcriptRef)
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   204→	if err := os.WriteFile(logFile, transcriptData, 0o600); err != nil {
   205→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   208→
   209→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on TurnStart)
   210→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   211→	if err != nil {
   212→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Determine transcript offset
   216→	transcriptOffset := resolveTranscriptOffset(preState, sessionID)
   217→
   218→	// Extract metadata via agent interface (prompts, summary, modified files)
   219→	var allPrompts []string
   220→	var summary string
   221→	var modifiedFiles []string
   222→	var newTranscriptPosition int
   223→
   224→	// Compute subagents directory for agents that support subagent extraction.
   225→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/
   226→	subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptRef), event.SessionID, "subagents")
   227→
   228→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   229→		// Extract prompts
   230→		if prompts, promptErr := analyzer.ExtractPrompts(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); promptErr != nil {
   231→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract prompts: %v\n", promptErr)
   232→		} else {
   233→			allPrompts = prompts
   234→		}
   235→
   236→		// Extract summary
   237→		if s, sumErr := analyzer.ExtractSummary(transcriptRef); sumErr != nil {
   238→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract summary: %v\n", sumErr)
   239→		} else {
   240→			summary = s
   241→		}
   242→
   243→		// Extract modified files - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor if available to include subagent files
   244→		if subagentExtractor, subOk := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); subOk {
   245→			if files, fileErr := subagentExtractor.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset, subagentsDir); fileErr != nil {
   246→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files (with subagents): %v\n", fileErr)
   247→			} else {
   248→				modifiedFiles = files
   249→			}
   250→			// Get position from basic analyzer
   251→			if _, pos, posErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); posErr == nil {
   252→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   253→			}
   254→		} else {
   255→			// Fall back to basic extraction (main transcript only)
   256→			if files, pos, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); fileErr != nil {
   257→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files: %v\n", fileErr)
   258→			} else {
   259→				modifiedFiles = files
   260→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   261→			}
   262→		}
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	// Write prompts file
   266→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   267→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   268→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   269→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   270→	}
   271→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   272→
   273→	// Write summary file
   274→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   275→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   276→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   277→	}
   278→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   279→
   280→	// Generate commit message from last prompt
   281→	lastPrompt := ""
   282→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   283→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   284→	}
   285→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   286→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   287→
   288→	// Get repo root for path normalization
   289→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   290→	if err != nil {
   291→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   292→	}
   293→
   294→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   295→	if preState != nil {
   296→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   297→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	// Detect file changes via git status
   301→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   302→	if err != nil {
   303→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	// Filter and normalize all paths
   307→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   308→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   309→	if changes != nil {
   310→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   311→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   312→	}
   313→
   314→	// Filter transcript-extracted files to exclude files already committed to HEAD.
   315→	// When an agent commits files mid-turn, those files are condensed by PostCommit
   316→	// and should not be re-added to FilesTouched by SaveStep. A file is "committed"
   317→	// if it exists in HEAD with the same content as the working tree.
   318→	relModifiedFiles = filterToUncommittedFiles(relModifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   319→
   320→	// Check if there are any changes
   321→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   322→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   323→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   324→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   325→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   326→		if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   327→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   328→		}
   329→		return nil
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	// Log file changes
   333→	logFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)
   334→
   335→	// Create context file
   336→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   337→	if err := createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, allPrompts, summary); err != nil {
   338→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   339→	}
   340→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   341→
   342→	// Get git author
   343→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   344→	if err != nil {
   345→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	// Get strategy and agent type
   349→	strat := GetStrategy()
   350→	agentType := ag.Type()
   351→
   352→	// Get transcript position/identifier from pre-prompt state
   353→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   354→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   355→	if preState != nil {
   356→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   357→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.TranscriptOffset
   358→	}
   359→
   360→	// Calculate token usage - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor to include subagent tokens
   361→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   362→	if subagentExtractor, ok := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); ok {
   363→		usage, tokenErr := subagentExtractor.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   364→		if tokenErr != nil {
   365→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage (with subagents): %v\n", tokenErr)
   366→		} else {
   367→			tokenUsage = usage
   368→		}
   369→	} else if calculator, ok := ag.(agent.TokenCalculator); ok {
   370→		// Fall back to basic token calculation (main transcript only)
   371→		usage, tokenErr := calculator.CalculateTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart)
   372→		if tokenErr != nil {
   373→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   374→		} else {
   375→			tokenUsage = usage
   376→		}
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	// Build fully-populated step context and delegate to strategy
   380→	ctx := strategy.StepContext{
   381→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   382→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   383→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   384→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   385→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   386→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   387→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   388→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptRef,
   389→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   390→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   391→		AgentType:                agentType,
   392→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   393→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   394→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   395→	}
   396→
   397→	if err := strat.SaveStep(ctx); err != nil {
   398→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save step: %w", err)
   399→	}
   400→
   401→	// Update session state transcript position for auto-commit strategy
   402→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && newTranscriptPosition > 0 {
   403→		updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID, newTranscriptPosition)
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	// Transition session phase and cleanup pre-prompt state
   407→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   408→	if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   409→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   410→	}
   411→
   412→	return nil
   413→}
   414→
   415→// handleLifecycleCompaction handles context compaction: saves current progress
   416→// but stays in ACTIVE phase (unlike TurnEnd which transitions to IDLE).
   417→// Also resets the transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated.
   418→func handleLifecycleCompaction(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   419→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   420→	logging.Info(logCtx, "compaction",
   421→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   422→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   423→	)
   424→
   425→	// Fire EventCompaction to trigger ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched (stays in ACTIVE)
   426→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   427→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   428→	if loadErr != nil {
   429→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for compaction: %v\n", loadErr)
   430→	}
   431→	if sessionState != nil {
   432→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(sessionState, session.EventCompaction, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   433→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: compaction transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   434→		}
   435→
   436→		// Reset transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated/reorganized
   437→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
   438→
   439→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); saveErr != nil {
   440→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save session state after compaction: %v\n", saveErr)
   441→		}
   442→	}
   443→
   444→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Context compaction: transcript offset reset\n")
   445→	return nil
   446→}
   447→
   448→// handleLifecycleSessionEnd handles session end: marks the session as ended.
   449→func handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   450→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   451→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   452→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   453→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   454→	)
   455→
   456→	if event.SessionID == "" {
   457→		return nil // No session to update
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	// Note: We intentionally don't clean up cached transcripts here.
   461→	// Post-session commits (carry-forward in ENDED phase) may still need
   462→	// the transcript to extract file changes. Cleanup is handled by
   463→	// `entire clean` or when the session state is fully removed.
   464→
   465→	if err := markSessionEnded(event.SessionID); err != nil {
   466→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   467→	}
   468→	return nil
   469→}
   470→
   471→// handleLifecycleSubagentStart handles subagent start: captures pre-task state.
   472→func handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   473→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   474→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-start",
   475→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   476→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   477→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   478→	)
   479→
   480→	// Log context
   481→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent started\n")
   482→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   483→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   484→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Transcript: %s\n", event.SessionRef)
   485→
   486→	// Capture pre-task state
   487→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(event.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   488→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   489→	}
   490→
   491→	return nil
   492→}
   493→
   494→// handleLifecycleSubagentEnd handles subagent end: detects changes, saves task checkpoint.
   495→func handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   496→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   497→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-end",
   498→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   499→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   500→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   501→		slog.String("subagent_id", event.SubagentID),
   502→	)
   503→
   504→	// Extract subagent type and description from tool input
   505→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(event.ToolInput)
   506→
   507→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   508→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(event.SessionRef)
   509→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   510→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   511→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, event.SubagentID)
   512→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   513→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   514→		}
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	// Log context
   518→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent completed\n")
   519→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   520→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   521→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   522→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Agent ID: %s\n", event.SubagentID)
   523→	}
   524→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   525→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Subagent Transcript: %s\n", subagentTranscriptPath)
   526→	}
   527→
   528→	// Extract modified files from subagent transcript
   529→	var modifiedFiles []string
   530→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   531→		transcriptToScan := event.SessionRef
   532→		if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   533→			transcriptToScan = subagentTranscriptPath
   534→		}
   535→		if files, _, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptToScan, 0); fileErr != nil {
   536→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files from subagent: %v\n", fileErr)
   537→		} else {
   538→			modifiedFiles = files
   539→		}
   540→	}
   541→
   542→	// Load pre-task state and detect file changes
   543→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID)
   544→	if err != nil {
   545→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   546→	}
   547→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   548→	if preState != nil {
   549→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   550→	}
   551→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   552→	if err != nil {
   553→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   554→	}
   555→
   556→	// Get repo root and normalize paths
   557→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   558→	if err != nil {
   559→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   560→	}
   561→
   562→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   563→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   564→	if changes != nil {
   565→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   566→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   567→	}
   568→
   569→	// If no changes, skip
   570→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   571→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   572→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   573→		return nil
   574→	}
   575→
   576→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort)
   577→	var checkpointUUID string
   578→	// Use the existing CLI-level checkpoint UUID finder
   579→	mainLines, _, _ := parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(event.SessionRef) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort
   580→	if mainLines != nil {
   581→		checkpointUUID, _ = FindCheckpointUUID(mainLines, event.ToolUseID)
   582→	}
   583→
   584→	// Get git author
   585→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   586→	if err != nil {
   587→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   588→	}
   589→
   590→	// Build task checkpoint context
   591→	strat := GetStrategy()
   592→	agentType := ag.Type()
   593→
   594→	ctx := strategy.TaskStepContext{
   595→		SessionID:              event.SessionID,
   596→		ToolUseID:              event.ToolUseID,
   597→		AgentID:                event.SubagentID,
   598→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   599→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   600→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   601→		TranscriptPath:         event.SessionRef,
   602→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   603→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   604→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   605→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   606→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   607→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   608→		AgentType:              agentType,
   609→	}
   610→
   611→	if err := strat.SaveTaskStep(ctx); err != nil {
   612→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task step: %w", err)
   613→	}
   614→
   615→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   616→	return nil
   617→}
   618→
   619→// --- Helper functions ---
   620→
   621→// resolveTranscriptOffset determines the transcript offset to use for parsing.
   622→// Prefers pre-prompt state, falls back to session state.
   623→func resolveTranscriptOffset(preState *PrePromptState, sessionID string) int {
   624→	if preState != nil && preState.TranscriptOffset > 0 {
   625→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", preState.TranscriptOffset)
   626→		return preState.TranscriptOffset
   627→	}
   628→
   629→	// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   630→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   631→	if loadErr != nil {
   632→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   633→		return 0
   634→	}
   635→	if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   636→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   637→		return sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   638→	}
   639→
   640→	return 0
   641→}
   642→
   643→// updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition updates the session state with the new transcript position
   644→// for the auto-commit strategy.
   645→func updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID string, newPosition int) {
   646→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   647→	if loadErr != nil {
   648→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   649→		return
   650→	}
   651→	if sessionState == nil {
   652→		sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   653→			SessionID: sessionID,
   654→		}
   655→	}
   656→	sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = newPosition
   657→	sessionState.StepCount++
   658→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   659→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   660→	} else {
   661→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   662→			newPosition, sessionState.StepCount)
   663→	}
   664→}
   665→
   666→// createContextFile creates a context.md file for the session checkpoint.
   667→// This is a unified version that works for all agents.
   668→func createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {
   669→	var sb strings.Builder
   670→
   671→	sb.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   672→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Session ID: %s\n", sessionID)
   673→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Commit Message: %s\n\n", commitMessage)
   674→
   675→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
   676→		sb.WriteString("## Prompts\n\n")
   677→		for i, p := range prompts {
   678→			fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "### Prompt %d\n\n%s\n\n", i+1, p)
   679→		}
   680→	}
   681→
   682→	if summary != "" {
   683→		sb.WriteString("## Summary\n\n")
   684→		sb.WriteString(summary)
   685→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   686→	}
   687→
   688→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   689→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   690→	}
   691→	return nil
   692→}
   693→
   694→// parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID is a thin wrapper around transcript parsing for checkpoint UUID lookup.
   695→// Returns parsed transcript lines for use with FindCheckpointUUID.
   696→func parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(transcriptPath string) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {
   697→	lines, total, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   698→	if err != nil {
   699→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("parsing transcript for checkpoint UUID: %w", err)
   700→	}
   701→	return lines, total, nil
   702→}
   703→
   704→// transitionSessionTurnEnd transitions the session phase to IDLE and dispatches turn-end actions.
   705→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   706→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   707→	if loadErr != nil {
   708→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   709→		return
   710→	}
   711→	if turnState == nil {
   712→		return
   713→	}
   714→	if err := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); err != nil {
   715→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end transition failed: %v\n", err)
   716→	}
   717→
   718→	// Always dispatch to strategy for turn-end handling. The strategy reads
   719→	// work items from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not the action list.
   720→	strat := GetStrategy()
   721→	if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   722→		if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState); err != nil {
   723→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   724→		}
   725→	}
   726→
   727→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   728→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   729→	}
   730→}
   731→
   732→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   733→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   734→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   735→	if err != nil {
   736→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   737→	}
   738→	if state == nil {
   739→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   740→	}
   741→
   742→	if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   743→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session stop transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   744→	}
   745→
   746→	now := time.Now()
   747→	state.EndedAt = &now
   748→
   749→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   750→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   751→	}
   752→	return nil
   753→}
   754→
   755→// logFileChanges logs the files modified, created, and deleted during a session.
   756→func logFileChanges(modified, newFiles, deleted []string) {
   757→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(modified))
   758→	for _, file := range modified {
   759→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   760→	}
   761→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   762→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(newFiles))
   763→		for _, file := range newFiles {
   764→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   765→		}
   766→	}
   767→	if len(deleted) > 0 {
   768→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(deleted))
   769→		for _, file := range deleted {
   770→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   771→		}
   772→	}
   773→}
   774→

   602→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
   603→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   604→
   605→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   606→	if err != nil {
   607→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   608→	}
   609→
   610→	// Get HEAD commit to check for trailer
   611→	head, err := repo.Head()
   612→	if err != nil {
   613→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   614→	}
   615→
   616→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   617→	if err != nil {
   618→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   619→	}
   620→
   621→	// Check if commit has checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
   622→	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
   623→	if !found {
   624→		// No trailer — user removed it or it was never added (mid-turn commit).
   625→		// Still update BaseCommit for active sessions so future commits can match.
   626→		s.postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly(logCtx, head)
   627→		return nil
   628→	}
   629→
   630→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   631→	if err != nil {
   632→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   636→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   637→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   638→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: no active sessions despite trailer",
   639→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   640→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   641→		)
   642→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   643→	}
   644→
   645→	// Build transition context
   646→	isRebase := isGitSequenceOperation()
   647→	transitionCtx := session.TransitionContext{
   648→		IsRebaseInProgress: isRebase,
   649→	}
   650→
   651→	if isRebase {
   652→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: rebase/sequence in progress, skipping phase transitions",
   653→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   654→		)
   655→	}
   656→
   657→	// Track shadow branch names and whether they can be deleted
   658→	shadowBranchesToDelete := make(map[string]struct{})
   659→	// Track active sessions that were NOT condensed — their shadow branches must be preserved
   660→	uncondensedActiveOnBranch := make(map[string]bool)
   661→
   662→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   663→	committedFileSet := filesChangedInCommit(commit)
   664→
   665→	for _, state := range sessions {
   666→		shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   667→
   668→		// Check for new content (needed for TransitionContext and condensation).
   669→		// Fail-open: if content check errors, assume new content exists so we
   670→		// don't silently skip data that should have been condensed.
   671→		//
   672→		// For ACTIVE sessions: the commit has a checkpoint trailer (verified above),
   673→		// meaning PrepareCommitMsg already determined this commit is session-related.
   674→		// The trailer is only added when either:
   675→		//   - No TTY (agent/subagent committing) — added unconditionally
   676→		//   - TTY (human committing) — added after content detection confirmed agent work
   677→		// In both cases, PrepareCommitMsg already validated this commit. We trust
   678→		// that decision here. Transcript-based re-validation is unreliable because
   679→		// subagent transcripts may not be available yet (subagent still running).
   680→		var hasNew bool
   681→		if state.Phase.IsActive() {
   682→			hasNew = true
   683→		} else {
   684→			var contentErr error
   685→			hasNew, contentErr = s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   686→			if contentErr != nil {
   687→				hasNew = true
   688→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: error checking session content, assuming new content",
   689→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   690→					slog.String("error", contentErr.Error()),
   691→				)
   692→			}
   693→		}
   694→		transitionCtx.HasFilesTouched = len(state.FilesTouched) > 0
   695→
   696→		// Save FilesTouched BEFORE TransitionAndLog — the handler's condensation
   697→		// clears it, but we need the original list for carry-forward computation.
   698→		// For mid-session commits (ACTIVE, no shadow branch), state.FilesTouched may be empty
   699→		// because no SaveStep/Stop has been called yet. Extract files from transcript.
   700→		filesTouchedBefore := make([]string, len(state.FilesTouched))
   701→		copy(filesTouchedBefore, state.FilesTouched)
   702→		if len(filesTouchedBefore) == 0 && state.Phase.IsActive() && state.TranscriptPath != "" {
   703→			filesTouchedBefore = s.extractFilesFromLiveTranscript(state)
   704→		}
   705→
   706→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward prep",
   707→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   708→			slog.Bool("is_active", state.Phase.IsActive()),
   709→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
   710→			slog.Int("files_touched_before", len(filesTouchedBefore)),
   711→			slog.Any("files", filesTouchedBefore),
   712→		)
   713→
   714→		// Run the state machine transition with handler for strategy-specific actions.
   715→		handler := &postCommitActionHandler{
   716→			s:                      s,
   717→			logCtx:                 logCtx,
   718→			repo:                   repo,
   719→			checkpointID:           checkpointID,
   720→			head:                   head,
   721→			commit:                 commit,
   722→			newHead:                newHead,
   723→			shadowBranchName:       shadowBranchName,
   724→			shadowBranchesToDelete: shadowBranchesToDelete,
   725→			committedFileSet:       committedFileSet,
   726→			hasNew:                 hasNew,
   727→			filesTouchedBefore:     filesTouchedBefore,
   728→		}
   729→
   730→		if err := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventGitCommit, transitionCtx, handler); err != nil {
   731→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: post-commit action handler error: %v\n", err)
   732→		}
   733→
   734→		// Record checkpoint ID for ACTIVE sessions so HandleTurnEnd can finalize
   735→		// with full transcript. IDLE/ENDED sessions already have complete transcripts.
   736→		// NOTE: This check runs AFTER TransitionAndLog updated the phase. It relies on
   737→		// ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE (phase stays ACTIVE). If that state machine
   738→		// transition ever changed, this guard would silently stop recording IDs.
   739→		if handler.condensed && state.Phase.IsActive() {
   740→			state.TurnCheckpointIDs = append(state.TurnCheckpointIDs, checkpointID.String())
   741→		}
   742→
   743→		// Carry forward remaining uncommitted files so the next commit gets its
   744→		// own checkpoint ID. This applies to ALL phases — if a user splits their
   745→		// commit across two `git commit` invocations, each gets a 1:1 checkpoint.
   746→		// Uses content-aware comparison: if user did `git add -p` and committed
   747→		// partial changes, the file still has remaining agent changes to carry forward.
   748→		if handler.condensed {
   749→			remainingFiles := filesWithRemainingAgentChanges(repo, shadowBranchName, commit, filesTouchedBefore, committedFileSet)
   750→			state.FilesTouched = remainingFiles
   751→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward decision (content-aware)",
   752→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   753→				slog.Int("files_touched_before", len(filesTouchedBefore)),
   754→				slog.Int("committed_files", len(committedFileSet)),
   755→				slog.Int("remaining_files", len(remainingFiles)),
   756→				slog.Any("remaining", remainingFiles),
   757→				slog.Any("committed_files", committedFileSet),
   758→			)
   759→			if len(remainingFiles) > 0 {
   760→				s.carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(logCtx, repo, state, remainingFiles)
   761→			}
   762→		}
   763→
   764→		// Save the updated state
   765→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   766→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   767→		}
   768→
   769→		// Only preserve shadow branch for active sessions that were NOT condensed.
   770→		// Condensed sessions already have their data on entire/checkpoints/v1.
   771→		if state.Phase.IsActive() && !handler.condensed {
   772→			uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true
   773→		}
   774→	}
   775→
   776→	// Clean up shadow branches — only delete when ALL sessions on the branch are non-active
   777→	// or were condensed during this PostCommit.
   778→	for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
   779→		if uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] {
   780→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: preserving shadow branch (active session exists)",
   781→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   782→			)
   783→			continue
   784→		}
   785→		if err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   786→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\n", shadowBranchName, err)
   787→		} else {
   788→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\n", shadowBranchName)
   789→			logging.Info(logCtx, "shadow branch deleted",
   790→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   791→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   792→			)
   793→		}
   794→	}
   795→
   796→	return nil
   797→}
   798→
   799→// condenseAndUpdateState runs condensation for a session and updates state afterward.
   800→// Returns true if condensation succeeded.
   801→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) condenseAndUpdateState(
```

   801→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) condenseAndUpdateState(
   802→	logCtx context.Context,
   803→	repo *git.Repository,
   804→	checkpointID id.CheckpointID,
   805→	state *SessionState,
   806→	head *plumbing.Reference,
   807→	shadowBranchName string,
   808→	shadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{},
   809→	committedFiles map[string]struct{},
   810→) bool {
   811→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state, committedFiles)
   812→	if err != nil {
   813→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: condensation failed for session %s: %v\n",
   814→			state.SessionID, err)
   815→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: condensation failed",
   816→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   817→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   818→		)
   819→		return false
   820→	}
   821→
   822→	// Track this shadow branch for cleanup
   823→	shadowBranchesToDelete[shadowBranchName] = struct{}{}
   824→
   825→	// Update session state for the new base commit
   826→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   827→	state.BaseCommit = newHead
   828→	state.AttributionBaseCommit = newHead
   829→	state.StepCount = 0
   830→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   831→
   832→	// Clear attribution tracking — condensation already used these values
   833→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   834→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   835→	state.FilesTouched = nil
   836→
   837→	// Save checkpoint ID so subsequent commits can reuse it (e.g., amend restores trailer)
   838→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   839→
   840→	shortID := state.SessionID
   841→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   842→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   843→	}
   844→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Condensed session %s: %s (%d checkpoints)\n",
   845→		shortID, result.CheckpointID, result.CheckpointsCount)
   846→
   847→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session condensed",
   848→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   849→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   850→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   851→		slog.Int("transcript_lines", result.TotalTranscriptLines),
   852→	)
   853→
   854→	return true
   855→}
   856→
   857→// updateBaseCommitIfChanged updates BaseCommit to newHead if it changed.
   858→// Only updates ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions should NOT have their
   859→// BaseCommit updated, as this would cause them to be incorrectly associated
   860→// with a new shadow branch and potentially condensed on future commits.
   861→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx context.Context, state *SessionState, newHead string) {
   862→	// Only update ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions are kept around for
   863→	// LastCheckpointID reuse and should not be advanced to HEAD.
   864→	if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   865→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updateBaseCommitIfChanged skipped non-active session",
   866→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   867→			slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   868→		)
   869→		return
   870→	}
   871→	if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   872→		state.BaseCommit = newHead
   873→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updated BaseCommit",
   874→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   875→			slog.String("new_head", truncateHash(newHead)),
   876→		)
   877→	}
   878→}
   879→
   880→// postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly updates BaseCommit for all sessions on the current
   881→// worktree when a commit has no Entire-Checkpoint trailer. This prevents BaseCommit
   882→// from going stale, which would cause future PrepareCommitMsg calls to skip the
   883→// session (BaseCommit != currentHeadHash filter).
   884→//
   885→// Unlike the full PostCommit flow, this does NOT fire EventGitCommit or trigger
   886→// condensation — it only keeps BaseCommit in sync with HEAD.
   887→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly(logCtx context.Context, head *plumbing.Reference) {
   888→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   889→	if err != nil {
   890→		return // Silent failure — hooks must be resilient
   891→	}
   892→
   893→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   894→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   895→		return
   896→	}
   897→
   898→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   899→	for _, state := range sessions {
   900→		// Only update active sessions. Idle/ended sessions are kept around for
   901→		// LastCheckpointID reuse and should not be advanced to HEAD.
   902→		if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   903→			continue
   904→		}
   905→		if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   906→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit (no trailer): updating BaseCommit",
   907→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   908→				slog.String("old_base", truncateHash(state.BaseCommit)),
   909→				slog.String("new_head", truncateHash(newHead)),
   910→			)
   911→			state.BaseCommit = newHead
   912→			if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   913→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   914→			}
   915→		}
   916→	}
   917→}

   920→func truncateHash(h string) string {
