```
     3→package session
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"log/slog"
     9→	"strings"
    10→	"time"
    11→
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    13→)
    14→
    15→// Phase represents the lifecycle stage of a session.
    16→type Phase string
    17→
    18→const (
    19→	PhaseActive Phase = "active"
    20→	PhaseIdle   Phase = "idle"
    21→	PhaseEnded  Phase = "ended"
    22→)
    23→
    24→// allPhases is the canonical list of phases for enumeration (e.g., diagram generation).
    25→var allPhases = []Phase{PhaseIdle, PhaseActive, PhaseEnded}
    26→
    27→// PhaseFromString normalizes a phase string, treating empty or unknown values
    28→// as PhaseIdle for backward compatibility with pre-state-machine session files.
    29→func PhaseFromString(s string) Phase {
    30→	switch Phase(s) {
    31→	case PhaseActive, "active_committed":
    32→		// "active_committed" was removed with the 1:1 checkpoint model.
    33→		// It previously meant "agent active + commit happened during turn".
    34→		// Normalize to ACTIVE so HandleTurnEnd can finalize any pending checkpoints.
    35→		return PhaseActive
    36→	case PhaseIdle:
    37→		return PhaseIdle
    38→	case PhaseEnded:
    39→		return PhaseEnded
    40→	default:
    41→		// Backward compat: truly unknown phases normalize to idle.
    42→		return PhaseIdle
    43→	}
    44→}
    45→
    46→// IsActive reports whether the phase represents an active agent turn.
    47→func (p Phase) IsActive() bool {
    48→	return p == PhaseActive
    49→}
    50→
    51→// Event represents something that happened to a session.
    52→type Event int
    53→
    54→const (
    55→	EventTurnStart    Event = iota // Agent begins working on a prompt
    56→	EventTurnEnd                   // Agent finishes its turn
    57→	EventGitCommit                 // A git commit was made (PostCommit hook)
    58→	EventSessionStart              // Session process started (SessionStart hook)
    59→	EventSessionStop               // Session process ended (SessionStop hook)
    60→	EventCompaction                // Agent compacted context mid-turn (PreCompress hook)
    61→)
    62→
    63→// allEvents is the canonical list of events for enumeration.
    64→var allEvents = []Event{EventTurnStart, EventTurnEnd, EventGitCommit, EventSessionStart, EventSessionStop, EventCompaction}
    65→
    66→// String returns a human-readable name for the event.
    67→func (e Event) String() string {
    68→	switch e {
    69→	case EventTurnStart:
    70→		return "TurnStart"
    71→	case EventTurnEnd:
    72→		return "TurnEnd"
    73→	case EventGitCommit:
    74→		return "GitCommit"
    75→	case EventSessionStart:
    76→		return "SessionStart"
    77→	case EventSessionStop:
    78→		return "SessionStop"
    79→	case EventCompaction:
    80→		return "Compaction"
    81→	default:
    82→		return fmt.Sprintf("Event(%d)", int(e))
    83→	}
    84→}
    85→
    86→// Action represents a side effect that should be performed after a transition.
    87→// The caller is responsible for executing these -- the state machine only declares them.
    88→type Action int
    89→
    90→const (
    91→	ActionCondense               Action = iota // Condense session data to permanent storage
    92→	ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched               // Condense only if FilesTouched is non-empty
    93→	ActionDiscardIfNoFiles                     // Discard session if FilesTouched is empty
    94→	ActionWarnStaleSession                     // Warn user about stale session(s)
    95→	ActionClearEndedAt                         // Clear EndedAt timestamp (session re-entering)
    96→	ActionUpdateLastInteraction                // Update LastInteractionTime
    97→)
    98→
    99→// String returns a human-readable name for the action.
   100→func (a Action) String() string {
   101→	switch a {
   102→	case ActionCondense:
   103→		return "Condense"
   104→	case ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched:
   105→		return "CondenseIfFilesTouched"
   106→	case ActionDiscardIfNoFiles:
   107→		return "DiscardIfNoFiles"
   108→	case ActionWarnStaleSession:
   109→		return "WarnStaleSession"
   110→	case ActionClearEndedAt:
   111→		return "ClearEndedAt"
   112→	case ActionUpdateLastInteraction:
   113→		return "UpdateLastInteraction"
   114→	default:
   115→		return fmt.Sprintf("Action(%d)", int(a))
   116→	}
   117→}
   118→
   119→// TransitionContext provides read-only context for transitions that need
   120→// to inspect session state without mutating it.
   121→type TransitionContext struct {
   122→	HasFilesTouched    bool // len(FilesTouched) > 0
   123→	IsRebaseInProgress bool // .git/rebase-merge/ or .git/rebase-apply/ exists
   124→}
   125→
   126→// TransitionResult holds the outcome of a state machine transition.
   127→type TransitionResult struct {
   128→	NewPhase Phase
   129→	Actions  []Action
   130→}
   131→
   132→// Transition computes the next phase and required actions given the current
   133→// phase and an event. This is a pure function with no side effects.
   134→//
   135→// Empty or unknown phase values are normalized to PhaseIdle for backward
   136→// compatibility with session state files created before phase tracking.
   137→func Transition(current Phase, event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   138→	// Normalize empty/unknown phase to idle for backward compatibility.
   139→	current = PhaseFromString(string(current))
   140→
   141→	switch current {
   142→	case PhaseIdle:
   143→		return transitionFromIdle(event, ctx)
   144→	case PhaseActive:
   145→		return transitionFromActive(event, ctx)
   146→	case PhaseEnded:
   147→		return transitionFromEnded(event, ctx)
   148→	default:
   149→		// PhaseFromString guarantees we only get known phases, but the
   150→		// exhaustive linter requires handling the default case.
   151→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   152→	}
   153→}
   154→
   155→func transitionFromIdle(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   156→	switch event {
   157→	case EventTurnStart:
   158→		return TransitionResult{
   159→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   160→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   161→		}
   162→	case EventTurnEnd:
   163→		// Turn end while idle is a no-op (no active turn to end).
   164→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   165→	case EventGitCommit:
   166→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   167→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   168→		}
   169→		return TransitionResult{
   170→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   171→			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   172→		}
   173→	case EventSessionStart:
   174→		// Already condensable, no-op.
   175→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   176→	case EventSessionStop:
   177→		return TransitionResult{
   178→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   179→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   180→		}
   181→	case EventCompaction:
   182→		// Compaction while idle shouldn't happen, but condense if there's work.
   183→		return TransitionResult{
   184→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   185→			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   186→		}
   187→	default:
   188→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
   189→	}
   190→}
   191→
   192→func transitionFromActive(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   193→	switch event {
   194→	case EventTurnStart:
   195→		// Ctrl-C recovery: agent crashed or user interrupted mid-turn.
   196→		return TransitionResult{
   197→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   198→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   199→		}
   200→	case EventTurnEnd:
   201→		return TransitionResult{
   202→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   203→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   204→		}
   205→	case EventGitCommit:
   206→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   207→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActive}
   208→		}
   209→		return TransitionResult{
   210→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   211→			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   212→		}
   213→	case EventSessionStart:
   214→		return TransitionResult{
   215→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   216→			Actions:  []Action{ActionWarnStaleSession},
   217→		}
   218→	case EventSessionStop:
   219→		return TransitionResult{
   220→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   221→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   222→		}
   223→	case EventCompaction:
   224→		// Compaction mid-turn: save current progress but stay active.
   225→		// The transcript offset will be reset by the compaction handler.
   226→		return TransitionResult{
   227→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   228→			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   229→		}
   230→	default:
   231→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActive}
   232→	}
   233→}
   234→
   235→func transitionFromEnded(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   236→	switch event {
   237→	case EventTurnStart:
   238→		return TransitionResult{
   239→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   240→			Actions:  []Action{ActionClearEndedAt, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   241→		}
   242→	case EventTurnEnd:
   243→		// Turn end while ended is a no-op.
   244→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   245→	case EventGitCommit:
   246→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   247→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   248→		}
   249→		if ctx.HasFilesTouched {
   250→			return TransitionResult{
   251→				NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   252→				Actions:  []Action{ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   253→			}
   254→		}
   255→		return TransitionResult{
   256→			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
   257→			Actions:  []Action{ActionDiscardIfNoFiles, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   258→		}
   259→	case EventSessionStart:
   260→		return TransitionResult{
   261→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   262→			Actions:  []Action{ActionClearEndedAt},
   263→		}
   264→	case EventSessionStop:
   265→		// Already ended, no-op.
   266→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   267→	case EventCompaction:
   268→		// Compaction while ended shouldn't happen, no-op.
   269→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   270→	default:
   271→		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
   272→	}
   273→}
   274→
   275→// ActionHandler defines strategy-specific side effects for state transitions.
   276→// The compiler enforces that every strategy-specific action has a handler.
   277→type ActionHandler interface {
   278→	HandleCondense(state *State) error
   279→	HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched(state *State) error
   280→	HandleDiscardIfNoFiles(state *State) error
   281→	HandleWarnStaleSession(state *State) error
   282→}
   283→
   284→// NoOpActionHandler is a default ActionHandler where all methods are no-ops.
   285→// Embed this in handler structs to only override the methods you need.
   286→type NoOpActionHandler struct{}
   287→
   288→func (NoOpActionHandler) HandleCondense(_ *State) error               { return nil }
   289→func (NoOpActionHandler) HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched(_ *State) error { return nil }
   290→func (NoOpActionHandler) HandleDiscardIfNoFiles(_ *State) error       { return nil }
   291→func (NoOpActionHandler) HandleWarnStaleSession(_ *State) error       { return nil }
   292→
   293→// ApplyTransition applies a TransitionResult to state: sets the new phase
   294→// unconditionally, then executes all actions. Common actions
   295→// (UpdateLastInteraction, ClearEndedAt) always run regardless of handler
   296→// errors so that bookkeeping fields stay consistent with the new phase.
   297→// Strategy-specific handler actions stop on the first error; subsequent
   298→// handler actions are skipped but common actions continue. Returns the
   299→// first handler error, or nil.
   300→func ApplyTransition(state *State, result TransitionResult, handler ActionHandler) error {
   301→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "session")
   302→
   303→	actionStrs := make([]string, len(result.Actions))
   304→	for i, a := range result.Actions {
   305→		actionStrs[i] = a.String()
   306→	}
   307→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "ApplyTransition",
   308→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   309→		slog.String("old_phase", string(state.Phase)),
   310→		slog.String("new_phase", string(result.NewPhase)),
   311→		slog.String("actions", strings.Join(actionStrs, ",")),
   312→	)
   313→
   314→	state.Phase = result.NewPhase
   315→
   316→	var handlerErr error
   317→	for _, action := range result.Actions {
   318→		switch action {
   319→		// Common actions: always applied, even after a handler error.
   320→		case ActionUpdateLastInteraction:
   321→			now := time.Now()
   322→			state.LastInteractionTime = &now
   323→		case ActionClearEndedAt:
   324→			state.EndedAt = nil
   325→
   326→		// Strategy-specific actions: skip remaining after the first handler error.
   327→		case ActionCondense:
   328→			if handlerErr == nil {
   329→				if err := handler.HandleCondense(state); err != nil {
   330→					handlerErr = fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", action, err)
   331→				}
   332→			}
   333→		case ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched:
   334→			if handlerErr == nil {
   335→				if err := handler.HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched(state); err != nil {
   336→					handlerErr = fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", action, err)
   337→				}
   338→			}
   339→		case ActionDiscardIfNoFiles:
   340→			if handlerErr == nil {
   341→				if err := handler.HandleDiscardIfNoFiles(state); err != nil {
   342→					handlerErr = fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", action, err)
   343→				}
   344→			}
   345→		case ActionWarnStaleSession:
   346→			if handlerErr == nil {
   347→				if err := handler.HandleWarnStaleSession(state); err != nil {
   348→					handlerErr = fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", action, err)
   349→				}
   350→			}
   351→		default:
   352→			if handlerErr == nil {
   353→				handlerErr = fmt.Errorf("unhandled action: %s", action)
   354→			}
   355→		}
   356→	}
   357→	return handlerErr
   358→}
   359→
   360→// MermaidDiagram generates a Mermaid state diagram from the transition table.
   361→// The diagram is derived by calling Transition() for representative context
   362→// variants per phase/event, so it stays in sync with the implementation.
   363→func MermaidDiagram() string {
   364→	var b strings.Builder
   365→	b.WriteString("stateDiagram-v2\n")
   366→
   367→	// State declarations with descriptions.
   368→	b.WriteString("    state \"IDLE\" as idle\n")
   369→	b.WriteString("    state \"ACTIVE\" as active\n")
   370→	b.WriteString("    state \"ENDED\" as ended\n")
   371→	b.WriteString("\n")
   372→
   373→	// Context variants for GitCommit: rebase, files/no-files.
   374→	type contextVariant struct {
   375→		label string
   376→		ctx   TransitionContext
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	// For each phase/event, generate edges.
   380→	for _, phase := range allPhases {
   381→		for _, event := range allEvents {
   382→			var variants []contextVariant
   383→			switch {
   384→			case event == EventGitCommit && phase == PhaseEnded:
   385→				// HasFilesTouched only affects PhaseEnded transitions;
   386→				// other phases produce identical edges for both values.
   387→				variants = []contextVariant{
   388→					{"[files]", TransitionContext{HasFilesTouched: true}},
   389→					{"[no files]", TransitionContext{HasFilesTouched: false}},
   390→					{"[rebase]", TransitionContext{IsRebaseInProgress: true}},
   391→				}
   392→			case event == EventGitCommit:
   393→				variants = []contextVariant{
   394→					{"", TransitionContext{}},
   395→					{"[rebase]", TransitionContext{IsRebaseInProgress: true}},
   396→				}
   397→			default:
   398→				variants = []contextVariant{
   399→					{"", TransitionContext{}},
   400→				}
   401→			}
   402→
   403→			for _, v := range variants {
   404→				result := Transition(phase, event, v.ctx)
   405→
   406→				// Build edge label.
   407→				label := event.String()
   408→				if v.label != "" {
   409→					label += " " + v.label
   410→				}
   411→				if len(result.Actions) > 0 {
   412→					actionNames := make([]string, 0, len(result.Actions))
   413→					for _, a := range result.Actions {
   414→						actionNames = append(actionNames, a.String())
   415→					}
   416→					label += " / " + strings.Join(actionNames, ", ")
   417→				}
   418→
   419→				from := string(phase)
   420→				to := string(result.NewPhase)
   421→
   422→				fmt.Fprintf(&b, "    %s --> %s : %s\n", from, to, label)
   423→			}
   424→		}
   425→	}
   426→
   427→	return b.String()
   428→}
   429→
```

```
     1→// Entire CLI plugin for OpenCode
     2→// Auto-generated by `entire enable --agent opencode`
     3→// Do not edit manually — changes will be overwritten on next install.
     4→// Requires Bun runtime (used by OpenCode's plugin system for loading ESM plugins).
     5→import type { Plugin } from "@opencode-ai/plugin"
     6→
     7→export const EntirePlugin: Plugin = async ({ $, directory }) => {
     8→  const ENTIRE_CMD = "__ENTIRE_CMD__"
     9→  // Track seen user messages to fire turn-start only once per message
    10→  const seenUserMessages = new Set<string>()
    11→  // Track current session ID for message events (which don't include sessionID)
    12→  let currentSessionID: string | null = null
    13→  // In-memory stores for message metadata
    14→  const messageStore = new Map<string, any>()
    15→  const partStore = new Map<string, any[]>()
    16→
    17→  /**
    18→   * Pipe JSON payload to an entire hooks command.
    19→   * Errors are logged but never thrown — plugin failures must not crash OpenCode.
    20→   */
    21→  async function callHook(hookName: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) {
    22→    try {
    23→      const json = JSON.stringify(payload)
    24→      await $`echo ${json} | ${ENTIRE_CMD} hooks opencode ${hookName}`.quiet().nothrow()
    25→    } catch {
    26→      // Silently ignore — plugin failures must not crash OpenCode
    27→    }
    28→  }
    29→
    30→  return {
    31→    event: async ({ event }) => {
    32→      switch (event.type) {
    33→        case "session.created": {
    34→          const session = (event as any).properties?.info
    35→          if (!session?.id) break
    36→          currentSessionID = session.id
    37→          await callHook("session-start", {
    38→            session_id: session.id,
    39→          })
    40→          break
    41→        }
    42→
    43→        case "message.updated": {
    44→          const msg = (event as any).properties?.info
    45→          if (!msg) break
    46→          // Store message metadata (role, time, tokens, etc.)
    47→          messageStore.set(msg.id, msg)
    48→          break
    49→        }
    50→
    51→        case "message.part.updated": {
    52→          const part = (event as any).properties?.part
    53→          if (!part?.messageID) break
    54→
    55→          // Accumulate parts per message
    56→          const existing = partStore.get(part.messageID) ?? []
    57→          const idx = existing.findIndex((p: any) => p.id === part.id)
    58→          if (idx >= 0) {
    59→            existing[idx] = part
    60→          } else {
    61→            existing.push(part)
    62→          }
    63→          partStore.set(part.messageID, existing)
    64→
    65→          // Fire turn-start on the first text part of a new user message
    66→          const msg = messageStore.get(part.messageID)
    67→          if (msg?.role === "user" && part.type === "text" && !seenUserMessages.has(msg.id)) {
    68→            seenUserMessages.add(msg.id)
    69→            const sessionID = msg.sessionID ?? currentSessionID
    70→            if (sessionID) {
    71→              await callHook("turn-start", {
    72→                session_id: sessionID,
    73→                prompt: part.text ?? "",
    74→              })
    75→            }
    76→          }
    77→          break
    78→        }
    79→
    80→        case "session.idle": {
    81→          const sessionID = (event as any).properties?.sessionID
    82→          if (!sessionID) break
    83→          // Go hook handler will call `opencode export` to get the transcript
    84→          await callHook("turn-end", {
    85→            session_id: sessionID,
    86→          })
    87→          break
    88→        }
    89→
    90→        case "session.compacted": {
    91→          const sessionID = (event as any).properties?.sessionID
    92→          if (!sessionID) break
    93→          await callHook("compaction", {
    94→            session_id: sessionID,
    95→          })
    96→          break
    97→        }
    98→
    99→        case "session.deleted": {
   100→          const session = (event as any).properties?.info
   101→          if (!session?.id) break
   102→          seenUserMessages.clear()
   103→          messageStore.clear()
   104→          partStore.clear()
   105→          currentSessionID = null
   106→          await callHook("session-end", {
   107→            session_id: session.id,
   108→          })
   109→          break
   110→        }
   111→      }
   112→    },
   113→  }
   114→}
   115→
```

```
     1→package opencode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"slices"
     8→	"strings"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// Compile-time interface assertions
    14→var (
    15→	_ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)
    16→	_ agent.TokenCalculator    = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)
    17→)
    18→
    19→// ParseExportSession parses export JSON content into an ExportSession structure.
    20→func ParseExportSession(data []byte) (*ExportSession, error) {
    21→	if len(data) == 0 {
    22→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil for empty data is expected
    23→	}
    24→
    25→	var session ExportSession
    26→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &session); err != nil {
    27→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse export session: %w", err)
    28→	}
    29→
    30→	return &session, nil
    31→}
    32→
    33→// parseExportSessionFromFile reads and parses an export JSON transcript file.
    34→func parseExportSessionFromFile(path string) (*ExportSession, error) {
    35→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Path from agent hook
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // Callers check os.IsNotExist on this error
    38→	}
    39→	return ParseExportSession(data)
    40→}
    41→
    42→// SliceFromMessage returns an OpenCode export transcript scoped to messages starting from
    43→// startMessageIndex. This is the OpenCode equivalent of transcript.SliceFromLine —
    44→// for OpenCode's JSON format, scoping is done by message index rather than line offset.
    45→// Returns the original data if startMessageIndex <= 0.
    46→// Returns nil if startMessageIndex exceeds the number of messages.
    47→func SliceFromMessage(data []byte, startMessageIndex int) []byte {
    48→	if len(data) == 0 || startMessageIndex <= 0 {
    49→		return data
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	session, err := ParseExportSession(data)
    53→	if err != nil || session == nil {
    54→		return nil
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	if startMessageIndex >= len(session.Messages) {
    58→		return nil
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	scoped := &ExportSession{
    62→		Info:     session.Info,
    63→		Messages: session.Messages[startMessageIndex:],
    64→	}
    65→
    66→	out, err := json.Marshal(scoped)
    67→	if err != nil {
    68→		return nil
    69→	}
    70→	return out
    71→}
    72→
    73→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the number of messages in the transcript.
    74→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
    75→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(path)
    76→	if err != nil {
    77→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
    78→			return 0, nil
    79→		}
    80→		return 0, err
    81→	}
    82→	if session == nil {
    83→		return 0, nil
    84→	}
    85→	return len(session.Messages), nil
    86→}
    87→
    88→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified by tool calls from the given message offset.
    89→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) ([]string, int, error) {
    90→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(path)
    91→	if err != nil {
    92→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
    93→			return nil, 0, nil
    94→		}
    95→		return nil, 0, err
    96→	}
    97→	if session == nil {
    98→		return nil, 0, nil
    99→	}
   100→
   101→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   102→	var files []string
   103→
   104→	for i := startOffset; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {
   105→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   106→		if msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant {
   107→			continue
   108→		}
   109→		for _, part := range msg.Parts {
   110→			if part.Type != "tool" || part.State == nil {
   111→				continue
   112→			}
   113→			if !slices.Contains(FileModificationTools, part.Tool) {
   114→				continue
   115→			}
   116→			filePath := extractFilePathFromInput(part.State.Input)
   117→			if filePath != "" && !seen[filePath] {
   118→				seen[filePath] = true
   119→				files = append(files, filePath)
   120→			}
   121→		}
   122→	}
   123→
   124→	return files, len(session.Messages), nil
   125→}
   126→
   127→// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts modified file paths from raw export JSON transcript bytes.
   128→// This is the bytes-based equivalent of ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset, used by ReadSession.
   129→func ExtractModifiedFiles(data []byte) ([]string, error) {
   130→	session, err := ParseExportSession(data)
   131→	if err != nil {
   132→		return nil, err
   133→	}
   134→	if session == nil {
   135→		return nil, nil
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   139→	var files []string
   140→
   141→	for _, msg := range session.Messages {
   142→		if msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant {
   143→			continue
   144→		}
   145→		for _, part := range msg.Parts {
   146→			if part.Type != "tool" || part.State == nil {
   147→				continue
   148→			}
   149→			if !slices.Contains(FileModificationTools, part.Tool) {
   150→				continue
   151→			}
   152→			filePath := extractFilePathFromInput(part.State.Input)
   153→			if filePath != "" && !seen[filePath] {
   154→				seen[filePath] = true
   155→				files = append(files, filePath)
   156→			}
   157→		}
   158→	}
   159→
   160→	return files, nil
   161→}
   162→
   163→// extractFilePathFromInput extracts the file path from a tool's input map.
   164→func extractFilePathFromInput(input map[string]any) string {
   165→	for _, key := range []string{"file_path", "path", "file", "filename"} {
   166→		if v, ok := input[key]; ok {
   167→			if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   168→				return s
   169→			}
   170→		}
   171→	}
   172→	return ""
   173→}
   174→
   175→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompt strings from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   176→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
   177→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(sessionRef)
   178→	if err != nil {
   179→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   180→			return nil, nil
   181→		}
   182→		return nil, err
   183→	}
   184→	if session == nil {
   185→		return nil, nil
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	var prompts []string
   189→	for i := fromOffset; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {
   190→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   191→		if msg.Info.Role != roleUser {
   192→			continue
   193→		}
   194→		// Extract text from parts
   195→		content := extractTextFromParts(msg.Parts)
   196→		if content != "" {
   197→			prompts = append(prompts, content)
   198→		}
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	return prompts, nil
   202→}
   203→
   204→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message content as a summary.
   205→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
   206→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(sessionRef)
   207→	if err != nil {
   208→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   209→			return "", nil
   210→		}
   211→		return "", err
   212→	}
   213→	if session == nil {
   214→		return "", nil
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	for i := len(session.Messages) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   218→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   219→		if msg.Info.Role == roleAssistant {
   220→			content := extractTextFromParts(msg.Parts)
   221→			if content != "" {
   222→				return content, nil
   223→			}
   224→		}
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	return "", nil
   228→}
   229→
   230→// extractTextFromParts extracts text content from message parts.
   231→func extractTextFromParts(parts []Part) string {
   232→	var texts []string
   233→	for _, part := range parts {
   234→		if part.Type == "text" && part.Text != "" {
   235→			texts = append(texts, part.Text)
   236→		}
   237→	}
   238→	return strings.Join(texts, "\n")
   239→}
   240→
   241→// ExtractAllUserPrompts extracts all user prompts from raw export JSON transcript bytes.
   242→// This is a package-level function used by the condensation path.
   243→func ExtractAllUserPrompts(data []byte) ([]string, error) {
   244→	session, err := ParseExportSession(data)
   245→	if err != nil {
   246→		return nil, err
   247→	}
   248→	if session == nil {
   249→		return nil, nil
   250→	}
   251→
   252→	var prompts []string
   253→	for _, msg := range session.Messages {
   254→		if msg.Info.Role != roleUser {
   255→			continue
   256→		}
   257→		content := extractTextFromParts(msg.Parts)
   258→		if content != "" {
   259→			prompts = append(prompts, content)
   260→		}
   261→	}
   262→	return prompts, nil
   263→}
   264→
   265→// CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes computes token usage from raw export JSON transcript bytes
   266→// starting at the given message offset.
   267→// This is a package-level function used by the condensation path (which has bytes, not a file path).
   268→func CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes(data []byte, startMessageIndex int) *agent.TokenUsage {
   269→	session, err := ParseExportSession(data)
   270→	if err != nil || session == nil {
   271→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   275→	for i := startMessageIndex; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {
   276→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   277→		if msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant || msg.Info.Tokens == nil {
   278→			continue
   279→		}
   280→		usage.InputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Input
   281→		usage.OutputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Output
   282→		usage.CacheReadTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Read
   283→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Write
   284→		usage.APICallCount++
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	return usage
   288→}
   289→
   290→// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from assistant messages starting at the given offset.
   291→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   292→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(sessionRef)
   293→	if err != nil {
   294→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   295→			return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil usage for nonexistent file is expected
   296→		}
   297→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript for token usage: %w", err)
   298→	}
   299→	if session == nil {
   300→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil usage for empty file is expected
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   304→	for i := fromOffset; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {
   305→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   306→		if msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant || msg.Info.Tokens == nil {
   307→			continue
   308→		}
   309→		usage.InputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Input
   310→		usage.OutputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Output
   311→		usage.CacheReadTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Read
   312→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Write
   313→		usage.APICallCount++
   314→	}
   315→
   316→	return usage, nil
   317→}
```

```
     1→package opencode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"errors"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"os/exec"
     8→	"strings"
     9→	"time"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// openCodeCommandTimeout is the maximum time to wait for opencode CLI commands.
    13→const openCodeCommandTimeout = 30 * time.Second
    14→
    15→// runOpenCodeSessionDelete runs `opencode session delete <sessionID>` to remove
    16→// a session from OpenCode's database. Treats "Session not found" as success
    17→// (nothing to delete).
    18→func runOpenCodeSessionDelete(sessionID string) error {
    19→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), openCodeCommandTimeout)
    20→	defer cancel()
    21→
    22→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "opencode", "session", "delete", sessionID)
    23→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
    24→	if err != nil {
    25→		if ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {
    26→			return fmt.Errorf("opencode session delete timed out after %s", openCodeCommandTimeout)
    27→		}
    28→		// Treat "Session not found" as success — nothing to delete.
    29→		if strings.Contains(string(output), "Session not found") {
    30→			return nil
    31→		}
    32→		return fmt.Errorf("opencode session delete failed: %w (output: %s)", err, string(output))
    33→	}
    34→	return nil
    35→}
    36→
    37→// runOpenCodeExport runs `opencode export <sessionID>` to export a session
    38→// from OpenCode's database. Returns the JSON export data as bytes.
    39→func runOpenCodeExport(sessionID string) ([]byte, error) {
    40→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), openCodeCommandTimeout)
    41→	defer cancel()
    42→
    43→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "opencode", "export", sessionID)
    44→	output, err := cmd.Output()
    45→	if err != nil {
    46→		if ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {
    47→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("opencode export timed out after %s", openCodeCommandTimeout)
    48→		}
    49→		// Get stderr for better error message
    50→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
    51→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
    52→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("opencode export failed: %w (stderr: %s)", err, string(exitErr.Stderr))
    53→		}
    54→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("opencode export failed: %w", err)
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	return output, nil
    58→}
    59→
    60→// runOpenCodeImport runs `opencode import <file>` to import a session into
    61→// OpenCode's database. The import preserves the original session ID
    62→// from the export file.
    63→func runOpenCodeImport(exportFilePath string) error {
    64→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), openCodeCommandTimeout)
    65→	defer cancel()
    66→
    67→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "opencode", "import", exportFilePath)
    68→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
    69→		if ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {
    70→			return fmt.Errorf("opencode import timed out after %s", openCodeCommandTimeout)
    71→		}
    72→		return fmt.Errorf("opencode import failed: %w (output: %s)", err, string(output))
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	return nil
    76→}
    77→
```

```
   219→
   220→// PrepareCommitMsg is called by the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
   221→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer to the commit message with a stable checkpoint ID.
   222→// Only adds a trailer if there's actually new session content to condense.
   223→// The actual condensation happens in PostCommit - if the user removes the trailer,
   224→// the commit will not be linked to the session (useful for "manual" commits).
   225→// For amended commits, preserves the existing checkpoint ID.
   226→//
   227→// The source parameter indicates how the commit was initiated:
   228→//   - "" or "template": normal editor flow - adds trailer with explanatory comment
   229→//   - "message": using -m or -F flag - prompts user interactively via /dev/tty
   230→//   - "merge", "squash": skip trailer entirely (auto-generated messages)
   231→//   - "commit": amend operation - preserves existing trailer or restores from LastCheckpointID
   232→//
   233→
   234→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source string) error {
   235→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   236→
   237→	// Skip during rebase, cherry-pick, or revert operations
   238→	// These are replaying existing commits and should not be linked to agent sessions
   239→	if isGitSequenceOperation() {
   240→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped during git sequence operation",
   241→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   242→			slog.String("source", source),
   243→		)
   244→		return nil
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	// Skip for merge and squash sources
   248→	// These are auto-generated messages - not from Claude sessions
   249→	switch source {
   250→	case "merge", "squash":
   251→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped for source",
   252→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   253→			slog.String("source", source),
   254→		)
   255→		return nil
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	// Handle amend (source="commit") separately: preserve or restore trailer
   259→	if source == "commit" {
   260→		return s.handleAmendCommitMsg(logCtx, commitMsgFile)
   261→	}
   262→
   263→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   264→	if err != nil {
   265→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   269→	if err != nil {
   270→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   271→	}
   272→
   273→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   274→	// We match by worktree (not BaseCommit) because the user may have made
   275→	// intermediate commits without entering new prompts, causing HEAD to diverge
   276→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   277→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   278→		// No active sessions or error listing - silently skip (hooks must be resilient)
   279→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no active sessions",
   280→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   281→			slog.String("source", source),
   282→		)
   283→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   284→	}
   285→
   286→	// Fast path: when an agent is committing (ACTIVE session + no TTY), skip
   287→	// content detection and interactive prompts. The agent can't respond to TTY
   288→	// prompts and the content detection can miss mid-session work (no shadow
   289→	// branch yet, transcript analysis may fail). Generate a checkpoint ID and
   290→	// add the trailer directly.
   291→	if !hasTTY() {
   292→		for _, state := range sessions {
   293→			if state.Phase.IsActive() {
   294→				return s.addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx, commitMsgFile, state, source)
   295→			}
   296→		}
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	// Check if any session has new content to condense
   300→	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
   301→
   302→	if len(sessionsWithContent) == 0 {
   303→		// No new content — no trailer needed
   304→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no content to link",
   305→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   306→			slog.String("source", source),
   307→			slog.Int("sessions_found", len(sessions)),
   308→		)
   309→		return nil
   310→	}
   311→
   312→	// Read current commit message
   313→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
   314→	if err != nil {
   315→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	message := string(content)
   319→
   320→	// Check if trailer already exists (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
   321→	if existingCpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
   322→		// Trailer already exists (e.g., amend) - keep it
   323→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer already exists",
   324→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   325→			slog.String("source", source),
   326→			slog.String("existing_checkpoint_id", existingCpID.String()),
   327→		)
   328→		return nil
   329→	}
   330→
   331→	// Generate a fresh checkpoint ID
   332→	checkpointID, err := id.Generate()
   333→	if err != nil {
   334→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   335→	}
   336→
   337→	// Determine agent type and last prompt from session
   338→	agentType := DefaultAgentType // default for backward compatibility
   339→	var lastPrompt string
   340→	if len(sessionsWithContent) > 0 {
   341→		firstSession := sessionsWithContent[0]
   342→		if firstSession.AgentType != "" {
   343→			agentType = firstSession.AgentType
   344→		}
   345→		lastPrompt = s.getLastPrompt(repo, firstSession)
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	// Prepare prompt for display: collapse newlines/whitespace, then truncate (rune-safe)
   349→	displayPrompt := stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(lastPrompt), 80, "...")
   350→
   351→	// Add trailer differently based on commit source
   352→	switch source {
   353→	case "message":
   354→		// Using -m or -F: ask user interactively whether to add trailer
   355→		// (comments won't be stripped by git in this mode)
   356→
   357→		// Build context string for interactive prompt
   358→		var promptContext string
   359→		if displayPrompt != "" {
   360→			promptContext = "You have an active " + string(agentType) + " session.\nLast Prompt: " + displayPrompt
   361→		}
   362→
   363→		if !askConfirmTTY("Link this commit to "+string(agentType)+" session context?", promptContext, true) {
   364→			// User declined - don't add trailer
   365→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: user declined trailer",
   366→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   367→				slog.String("source", source),
   368→			)
   369→			return nil
   370→		}
   371→		message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, checkpointID)
   372→	default:
   373→		// Normal editor flow: add trailer with explanatory comment (will be stripped by git)
   374→		message = addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message, checkpointID, string(agentType), displayPrompt)
   375→	}
   376→
   377→	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer added",
   378→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   379→		slog.String("source", source),
   380→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   381→	)
   382→
   383→	// Write updated message back
   384→	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
   385→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	return nil
   389→}
   390→
   391→// handleAmendCommitMsg handles the prepare-commit-msg hook for amend operations
   392→// (source="commit"). It preserves existing trailers or restores from LastCheckpointID.
   393→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) handleAmendCommitMsg(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string) error {
   394→	// Read current commit message
   395→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
   396→	if err != nil {
   397→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   398→	}
   399→
```

```
  1045→	)
  1046→
  1047→	if !hasTranscriptGrowth && !hasUncommittedFiles {
  1048→		return false, nil // No new content and no carry-forward files
  1049→	}
  1050→
  1051→	// Check if staged files overlap with session's files with content-aware matching.
  1052→	// This is primarily for PrepareCommitMsg; in PostCommit, stagedFiles is empty.

  1066→	// if there's no transcript growth, the session hasn't done new work since last checkpoint.
  1067→	// (Carry-forward creates a shadow branch WITHOUT transcript, handled in the block above.)
  1068→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no staged files, returning transcript growth",
  1069→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1070→		slog.Bool("has_transcript_growth", hasTranscriptGrowth),
  1071→		slog.Bool("has_uncommitted_files", hasUncommittedFiles),
  1072→	)
  1073→	return hasTranscriptGrowth, nil
  1074→}

  1076→// sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript checks if a session has new content
  1077→// by examining the live transcript file. This is used when no shadow branch exists
  1078→// (i.e., no Stop has happened yet) but the agent may have done work.
  1079→//
  1080→// Returns true if:
  1081→//  1. The transcript has grown since the last condensation, AND
  1082→//  2. The new transcript portion contains file modifications, AND
  1083→//  3. At least one modified file overlaps with the currently staged files
  1084→//
  1085→// The overlap check ensures we don't add checkpoint trailers to commits that are
  1086→// unrelated to the agent's recent changes.

  1088→// This handles the scenario where the agent commits mid-session before Stop.
```

```
    11→)
    12→
    13→// TestOpenCodeHookFlow verifies the full hook flow for OpenCode:
    14→// session-start → turn-start → file changes → turn-end → checkpoint → commit → condense → session-end.
    15→func TestOpenCodeHookFlow(t *testing.T) {
    16→	t.Parallel()
    17→
    18→	RunForAllStrategies(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
    19→		env.InitEntireWithAgent(strategyName, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)
    20→
    21→		// Create OpenCode session
    22→		session := env.NewOpenCodeSession()
    23→
    24→		// 1. session-start
    25→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    26→			t.Fatalf("session-start error: %v", err)
    27→		}
    28→
    29→		// 2. turn-start (equivalent to UserPromptSubmit — captures pre-prompt state)
    30→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath, "Add a feature"); err != nil {
    31→			t.Fatalf("turn-start error: %v", err)
    32→		}
    33→
    34→		// 3. Agent makes file changes (AFTER turn-start so they're detected as new)
    35→		env.WriteFile("feature.go", "package main\n// new feature")
    36→
    37→		// 4. Create transcript with the file change
    38→		session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Add a feature", []FileChange{
    39→			{Path: "feature.go", Content: "package main\n// new feature"},
    40→		})
    41→
    42→		// 5. turn-end (equivalent to Stop — creates checkpoint)
    43→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    44→			t.Fatalf("turn-end error: %v", err)
    45→		}
    46→
    47→		// 6. Verify checkpoint was created
    48→		points := env.GetRewindPoints()
    49→		if len(points) == 0 {
    50→			t.Fatal("expected at least 1 rewind point after turn-end")
    51→		}
    52→
    53→		// 7. For manual-commit, user commits manually (triggers condensation).
    54→		// For auto-commit, the commit was already made during turn-end.
    55→		if strategyName == strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit {
    56→			env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add feature", "feature.go")
    57→		}
    58→
    59→		// 8. session-end
    60→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionEnd(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    61→			t.Fatalf("session-end error: %v", err)
    62→		}
    63→
    64→		// 9. Verify condensation happened (checkpoint on metadata branch)
    65→		checkpointID := env.TryGetLatestCheckpointID()
    66→		if checkpointID == "" {
    67→			t.Fatal("expected checkpoint on metadata branch after commit")
    68→		}
    69→
    70→		// 10. Verify condensed data
    71→		transcriptPath := SessionFilePath(checkpointID, paths.TranscriptFileName)
    72→		_, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, transcriptPath)
    73→		if !found {
    74→			t.Error("condensed transcript should exist on metadata branch")
    75→		}
    76→	})
    77→}
    78→
    79→// TestOpenCodeAgentStrategyComposition verifies that the OpenCode agent and strategy
    80→// work together correctly — agent parses session, strategy saves checkpoint, rewind works.
    81→func TestOpenCodeAgentStrategyComposition(t *testing.T) {
    82→	t.Parallel()
    83→
    84→	RunForAllStrategies(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
    85→		env.InitEntireWithAgent(strategyName, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)
    86→
    87→		ag, err := agent.Get("opencode")
    88→		if err != nil {
    89→			t.Fatalf("Get(opencode) error = %v", err)
    90→		}
    91→
    92→		_, err = strategy.Get(strategyName)
    93→		if err != nil {
    94→			t.Fatalf("Get(%s) error = %v", strategyName, err)
    95→		}
    96→
    97→		// Create session and transcript for agent interface testing.
    98→		// The transcript references feature.go but the actual file doesn't need
    99→		// to exist for ReadSession — it only parses the transcript JSONL.
   100→		session := env.NewOpenCodeSession()
   101→		transcriptPath := session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Add a feature", []FileChange{
   102→			{Path: "feature.go", Content: "package main\n// new feature"},
   103→		})
   104→
   105→		// Read session via agent interface
   106→		agentSession, err := ag.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   107→			SessionID:  session.ID,
   108→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   109→		})
   110→		if err != nil {
   111→			t.Fatalf("ReadSession() error = %v", err)
   112→		}
   113→
   114→		// Verify agent computed modified files
   115→		if len(agentSession.ModifiedFiles) == 0 {
   116→			t.Error("agent.ReadSession() should compute ModifiedFiles")
   117→		}
   118→
   119→		// Simulate session flow: session-start → turn-start → file changes → turn-end
   120→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   121→			t.Fatalf("session-start error = %v", err)
   122→		}
   123→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Add a feature"); err != nil {
   124→			t.Fatalf("turn-start error = %v", err)
   125→		}
   126→
   127→		// Create the actual file AFTER turn-start so the strategy detects it as new
   128→		env.WriteFile("feature.go", "package main\n// new feature")
   129→
   130→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   131→			t.Fatalf("turn-end error = %v", err)
   132→		}
   133→
   134→		// Verify checkpoint was created
   135→		points := env.GetRewindPoints()
   136→		if len(points) == 0 {
   137→			t.Fatal("expected at least 1 rewind point after turn-end")
   138→		}
   139→	})
   140→}
   141→
   142→// TestOpenCodeRewind verifies that rewind works with OpenCode checkpoints.
   143→func TestOpenCodeRewind(t *testing.T) {
   144→	t.Parallel()
   145→
   146→	// Test with manual-commit strategy as it has full file restoration on rewind
   147→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   148→	env.InitEntireWithAgent(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)
   149→
   150→	// First session
   151→	session := env.NewOpenCodeSession()
   152→	transcriptPath := session.TranscriptPath
   153→
   154→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   155→		t.Fatalf("session-start error: %v", err)
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Turn 1: create file1.go (AFTER turn-start so it's detected as new)
   159→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Create file1"); err != nil {
   160→		t.Fatalf("turn-start error: %v", err)
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	env.WriteFile("file1.go", "package main\n// file1 v1")
   164→	session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Create file1", []FileChange{
   165→		{Path: "file1.go", Content: "package main\n// file1 v1"},
   166→	})
   167→
   168→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   169→		t.Fatalf("turn-end error: %v", err)
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	points1 := env.GetRewindPoints()
   173→	if len(points1) == 0 {
   174→		t.Fatal("no rewind point after first turn")
   175→	}
   176→	checkpoint1ID := points1[0].ID
   177→
   178→	// Turn 2: modify file1 and create file2 (AFTER turn-start)
   179→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Modify file1"); err != nil {
   180→		t.Fatalf("turn-start error: %v", err)
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	env.WriteFile("file1.go", "package main\n// file1 v2")
   184→	env.WriteFile("file2.go", "package main\n// file2")
   185→	session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Modify file1, create file2", []FileChange{
   186→		{Path: "file1.go", Content: "package main\n// file1 v2"},
   187→		{Path: "file2.go", Content: "package main\n// file2"},
   188→	})
   189→
   190→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   191→		t.Fatalf("turn-end error: %v", err)
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Verify 2 checkpoints
   195→	points2 := env.GetRewindPoints()
   196→	if len(points2) < 2 {
   197→		t.Fatalf("expected at least 2 rewind points, got %d", len(points2))
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	// Rewind to first checkpoint
   201→	if err := env.Rewind(checkpoint1ID); err != nil {
   202→		t.Fatalf("Rewind() error = %v", err)
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	// Verify file1 is restored to v1
   206→	content := env.ReadFile("file1.go")
   207→	if content != "package main\n// file1 v1" {
   208→		t.Errorf("file1.go after rewind = %q, want v1 content", content)
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	// file2 should not exist after rewind to checkpoint 1
   212→	if env.FileExists("file2.go") {
   213→		t.Error("file2.go should not exist after rewind to checkpoint 1")
   214→	}
   215→}
   216→
   217→// TestOpenCodeMultiTurnCondensation verifies that multiple turns in a session
   218→// are correctly condensed when the user commits.
   219→func TestOpenCodeMultiTurnCondensation(t *testing.T) {
   220→	t.Parallel()
   221→
   222→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   223→	env.InitEntireWithAgent(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)
   224→
   225→	session := env.NewOpenCodeSession()
   226→	transcriptPath := session.TranscriptPath
   227→
   228→	// session-start
   229→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   230→		t.Fatalf("session-start error: %v", err)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	// Turn 1: create file (AFTER turn-start so it's detected as new)
   234→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Create app.go"); err != nil {
   235→		t.Fatalf("turn-start error: %v", err)
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	env.WriteFile("app.go", "package main\nfunc main() {}")
   239→	session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Create app.go", []FileChange{
   240→		{Path: "app.go", Content: "package main\nfunc main() {}"},
   241→	})
   242→
   243→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   244→		t.Fatalf("turn-end error: %v", err)
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	// Verify checkpoint
   248→	points := env.GetRewindPoints()
   249→	if len(points) == 0 {
   250→		t.Fatal("expected rewind point after first turn")
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Commit with hooks (triggers condensation)
   254→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Implement app", "app.go")
   255→
   256→	// session-end
   257→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   258→		t.Fatalf("session-end error: %v", err)
   259→	}
   260→
   261→	// Verify checkpoint was condensed to metadata branch
   262→	checkpointID := env.TryGetLatestCheckpointID()
   263→	if checkpointID == "" {
   264→		t.Fatal("expected checkpoint on metadata branch after commit")
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	// Verify files are on metadata branch
   268→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   269→		CheckpointID: checkpointID,
   270→		Strategy:     strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit,
   271→		FilesTouched: []string{"app.go"},
   272→		ExpectedTranscriptContent: []string{
   273→			"Create app.go", // User prompt should appear in transcript
   274→		},
   275→	})
   276→}
   277→
```

```
     1→package opencode
     2→
     3→// sessionInfoRaw matches the JSON payload piped from the OpenCode plugin for session events.
     4→// The plugin sends only session_id; Go calls `opencode export` to get the transcript.
     5→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
     6→	SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
     7→}
     8→
     9→// turnStartRaw matches the JSON payload for turn-start (user prompt submission).
    10→type turnStartRaw struct {
    11→	SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
    12→	Prompt    string `json:"prompt"`
    13→}
    14→
    15→// --- Export JSON types (from `opencode export`) ---
    16→
    17→// ExportSession represents the top-level structure of `opencode export` output.
    18→// This is OpenCode's native format for session data.
    19→type ExportSession struct {
    20→	Info     SessionInfo     `json:"info"`
    21→	Messages []ExportMessage `json:"messages"`
    22→}
    23→
    24→// SessionInfo contains session metadata from the export.
    25→type SessionInfo struct {
    26→	ID        string `json:"id"`
    27→	Title     string `json:"title,omitempty"`
    28→	CreatedAt int64  `json:"createdAt,omitempty"`
    29→	UpdatedAt int64  `json:"updatedAt,omitempty"`
    30→}
    31→
    32→// ExportMessage represents a single message in the export format.
    33→// Each message contains info (metadata) and parts (content).
    34→type ExportMessage struct {
    35→	Info  MessageInfo `json:"info"`
    36→	Parts []Part      `json:"parts"`
    37→}
    38→
    39→// MessageInfo contains message metadata.
    40→type MessageInfo struct {
    41→	ID        string  `json:"id"`
    42→	SessionID string  `json:"sessionID,omitempty"`
    43→	Role      string  `json:"role"` // "user" or "assistant"
    44→	Time      Time    `json:"time"`
    45→	Tokens    *Tokens `json:"tokens,omitempty"`
    46→	Cost      float64 `json:"cost,omitempty"`
    47→}
    48→
    49→// Message role constants.
    50→const (
    51→	roleAssistant = "assistant"
    52→	roleUser      = "user"
    53→)
    54→
    55→// Time holds message timestamps.
    56→type Time struct {
    57→	Created   int64 `json:"created"`
    58→	Completed int64 `json:"completed,omitempty"`
    59→}
    60→
    61→// Tokens holds token usage from assistant messages.
    62→type Tokens struct {
    63→	Input     int   `json:"input"`
    64→	Output    int   `json:"output"`
    65→	Reasoning int   `json:"reasoning"`
    66→	Cache     Cache `json:"cache"`
    67→}
    68→
    69→// Cache holds cache-related token counts.
    70→type Cache struct {
    71→	Read  int `json:"read"`
    72→	Write int `json:"write"`
    73→}
    74→
    75→// Part represents a message part (text, tool, etc.).
    76→type Part struct {
    77→	Type   string     `json:"type"` // "text", "tool", etc.
    78→	Text   string     `json:"text,omitempty"`
    79→	Tool   string     `json:"tool,omitempty"`
    80→	CallID string     `json:"callID,omitempty"`
    81→	State  *ToolState `json:"state,omitempty"`
    82→}
    83→
    84→// ToolState represents tool execution state.
    85→type ToolState struct {
    86→	Status string         `json:"status"` // "pending", "running", "completed", "error"
    87→	Input  map[string]any `json:"input,omitempty"`
    88→	Output string         `json:"output,omitempty"`
    89→}
    90→
    91→// FileModificationTools are tools in OpenCode that modify files on disk.
    92→// These match the actual tool names from OpenCode's source:
    93→//   - edit:  internal/llm/tools/edit.go  (EditToolName)
    94→//   - write: internal/llm/tools/write.go (WriteToolName)
    95→//   - patch: internal/llm/tools/patch.go (PatchToolName)
    96→var FileModificationTools = []string{
    97→	"edit",
    98→	"write",
    99→	"patch",
   100→}
   101→
```

```
   150→// handleLifecycleTurnEnd handles turn end: validates transcript, extracts metadata,
   151→// detects file changes, saves step + checkpoint, transitions phase.
   152→//
   153→//nolint:maintidx // high complexity due to sequential orchestration of 8 steps (validation, extraction, file detection, filtering, token calc, step save, phase transition, cleanup) - splitting would obscure the flow
   154→func handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   155→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   156→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-end",
   157→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   158→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   159→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   160→	)
   161→
   162→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   163→	if sessionID == "" {
   164→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	transcriptRef := event.SessionRef
   168→	if transcriptRef == "" {
   169→		return errors.New("transcript file not specified")
   170→	}
   171→	if !fileExists(transcriptRef) {
   172→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found: %s", transcriptRef)
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   176→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   177→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   178→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Create session metadata directory
   182→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   183→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir
   186→	}
   187→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   188→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// If agent implements TranscriptPreparer, wait for transcript to be ready
   192→	if preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {
   193→		if err := preparer.PrepareTranscript(transcriptRef); err != nil {
   194→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to prepare transcript: %v\n", err)
   195→		}
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Copy transcript to session directory
   199→	transcriptData, err := ag.ReadTranscript(transcriptRef)
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   204→	if err := os.WriteFile(logFile, transcriptData, 0o600); err != nil {
   205→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   208→
   209→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on TurnStart)
   210→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   211→	if err != nil {
   212→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Determine transcript offset
   216→	transcriptOffset := resolveTranscriptOffset(preState, sessionID)
   217→
   218→	// Extract metadata via agent interface (prompts, summary, modified files)
   219→	var allPrompts []string
   220→	var summary string
   221→	var modifiedFiles []string
   222→	var newTranscriptPosition int
   223→
   224→	// Compute subagents directory for agents that support subagent extraction.
   225→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/
   226→	subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptRef), event.SessionID, "subagents")
   227→
   228→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   229→		// Extract prompts
   230→		if prompts, promptErr := analyzer.ExtractPrompts(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); promptErr != nil {
   231→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract prompts: %v\n", promptErr)
   232→		} else {
   233→			allPrompts = prompts
   234→		}
   235→
   236→		// Extract summary
   237→		if s, sumErr := analyzer.ExtractSummary(transcriptRef); sumErr != nil {
   238→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract summary: %v\n", sumErr)
   239→		} else {
   240→			summary = s
   241→		}
   242→
   243→		// Extract modified files - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor if available to include subagent files
   244→		if subagentExtractor, subOk := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); subOk {
   245→			if files, fileErr := subagentExtractor.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset, subagentsDir); fileErr != nil {
   246→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files (with subagents): %v\n", fileErr)
   247→			} else {
   248→				modifiedFiles = files
   249→			}
   250→			// Get position from basic analyzer
   251→			if _, pos, posErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); posErr == nil {
   252→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   253→			}
   254→		} else {
   255→			// Fall back to basic extraction (main transcript only)
   256→			if files, pos, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); fileErr != nil {
   257→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files: %v\n", fileErr)
   258→			} else {
   259→				modifiedFiles = files
   260→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   261→			}
   262→		}
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	// Write prompts file
   266→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   267→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   268→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   269→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   270→	}
   271→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   272→
   273→	// Write summary file
   274→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   275→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   276→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   277→	}
   278→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   279→
   280→	// Generate commit message from last prompt
   281→	lastPrompt := ""
   282→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   283→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   284→	}
   285→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   286→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   287→
   288→	// Get repo root for path normalization
   289→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   290→	if err != nil {
   291→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   292→	}
   293→
   294→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   295→	if preState != nil {
   296→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   297→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	// Detect file changes via git status
   301→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   302→	if err != nil {
   303→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	// Filter and normalize all paths
   307→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   308→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   309→	if changes != nil {
   310→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   311→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   312→	}
   313→
   314→	// Filter transcript-extracted files to exclude files already committed to HEAD.
   315→	// When an agent commits files mid-turn, those files are condensed by PostCommit
   316→	// and should not be re-added to FilesTouched by SaveStep. A file is "committed"
   317→	// if it exists in HEAD with the same content as the working tree.
   318→	relModifiedFiles = filterToUncommittedFiles(relModifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   319→
   320→	// Check if there are any changes
   321→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   322→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   323→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   324→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   325→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   326→		if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   327→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   328→		}
   329→		return nil
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	// Log file changes
   333→	logFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)
   334→
   335→	// Create context file
   336→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   337→	if err := createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, allPrompts, summary); err != nil {
   338→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   339→	}
   340→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   341→
   342→	// Get git author
   343→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   344→	if err != nil {
   345→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	// Get strategy and agent type
   349→	strat := GetStrategy()
   350→	agentType := ag.Type()
   351→
   352→	// Get transcript position/identifier from pre-prompt state
   353→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   354→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   355→	if preState != nil {
   356→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   357→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.TranscriptOffset
   358→	}
   359→
   360→	// Calculate token usage - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor to include subagent tokens
   361→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   362→	if subagentExtractor, ok := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); ok {
   363→		usage, tokenErr := subagentExtractor.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   364→		if tokenErr != nil {
   365→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage (with subagents): %v\n", tokenErr)
   366→		} else {
   367→			tokenUsage = usage
   368→		}
   369→	} else if calculator, ok := ag.(agent.TokenCalculator); ok {
   370→		// Fall back to basic token calculation (main transcript only)
   371→		usage, tokenErr := calculator.CalculateTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart)
   372→		if tokenErr != nil {
   373→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   374→		} else {
   375→			tokenUsage = usage
   376→		}
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	// Build fully-populated step context and delegate to strategy
   380→	ctx := strategy.StepContext{
   381→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   382→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   383→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   384→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   385→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   386→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   387→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
```

```
     1→package opencode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"io"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"path/filepath"
     8→	"time"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// Hook name constants — these become CLI subcommands under `entire hooks opencode`.
    15→const (
    16→	HookNameSessionStart = "session-start"
    17→	HookNameSessionEnd   = "session-end"
    18→	HookNameTurnStart    = "turn-start"
    19→	HookNameTurnEnd      = "turn-end"
    20→	HookNameCompaction   = "compaction"
    21→)
    22→
    23→// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    24→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) HookNames() []string {
    25→	return []string{
    26→		HookNameSessionStart,
    27→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    28→		HookNameTurnStart,
    29→		HookNameTurnEnd,
    30→		HookNameCompaction,
    31→	}
    32→}
    33→
    34→// ParseHookEvent translates OpenCode hook calls into normalized lifecycle events.
    35→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    36→	switch hookName {
    37→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    38→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    39→		if err != nil {
    40→			return nil, err
    41→		}
    42→		return &agent.Event{
    43→			Type:      agent.SessionStart,
    44→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
    45→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
    46→		}, nil
    47→
    48→	case HookNameTurnStart:
    49→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[turnStartRaw](stdin)
    50→		if err != nil {
    51→			return nil, err
    52→		}
    53→		// Get the temp file path for this session (may not exist yet, but needed for pre-prompt state).
    54→		// Repo root may fail if called outside a repo (unlikely in hook context).
    55→		// Fallback to "." is acceptable - the file will still be written, just
    56→		// to current directory instead of .entire/tmp/.
    57→		repoRoot, _ := paths.RepoRoot() //nolint:errcheck // see comment above
    58→		tmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)
    59→		transcriptPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, raw.SessionID+".json")
    60→		return &agent.Event{
    61→			Type:       agent.TurnStart,
    62→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
    63→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
    64→			Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
    65→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
    66→		}, nil
    67→
    68→	case HookNameTurnEnd:
    69→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    70→		if err != nil {
    71→			return nil, err
    72→		}
    73→		// Call `opencode export` to get the transcript and write to temp file
    74→		transcriptPath, exportErr := a.fetchAndCacheExport(raw.SessionID)
    75→		if exportErr != nil {
    76→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to export session: %w", exportErr)
    77→		}
    78→		return &agent.Event{
    79→			Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
    80→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
    81→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
    82→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
    83→		}, nil
    84→
    85→	case HookNameCompaction:
    86→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    87→		if err != nil {
    88→			return nil, err
    89→		}
    90→		return &agent.Event{
    91→			Type:      agent.Compaction,
    92→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
    93→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
    94→		}, nil
    95→
    96→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    97→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    98→		if err != nil {
    99→			return nil, err
   100→		}
   101→		return &agent.Event{
   102→			Type:      agent.SessionEnd,
   103→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
   104→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
   105→		}, nil
   106→
   107→	default:
   108→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action for unknown hooks
   109→	}
   110→}
   111→
   112→// fetchAndCacheExport calls `opencode export <sessionID>` and writes the result
   113→// to a temporary file. Returns the path to the temp file.
   114→//
   115→// Integration testing: Set ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT=1 to skip the
   116→// `opencode export` call and use pre-written mock data instead. Tests must
   117→// pre-write the transcript file to .entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json before
   118→// triggering the hook. See integration_test/hooks.go:SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd.
   119→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) fetchAndCacheExport(sessionID string) (string, error) {
   120→	// Get repo root for the temp directory
   121→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   122→	if err != nil {
   123→		repoRoot = "."
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	tmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)
   127→	tmpFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, sessionID+".json")
   128→
   129→	// Integration test mode: use pre-written mock file without calling opencode export
   130→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT") != "" {
   131→		if _, err := os.Stat(tmpFile); err == nil {
   132→			return tmpFile, nil
   133→		}
   134→		return "", fmt.Errorf("mock export file not found: %s (ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT is set)", tmpFile)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Call opencode export to get the transcript (always refresh on each turn)
   138→	data, err := runOpenCodeExport(sessionID)
   139→	if err != nil {
   140→		return "", fmt.Errorf("opencode export failed: %w", err)
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	// Write to temp directory under .entire
   144→	if err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   145→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create temp dir: %w", err)
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   149→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to write export file: %w", err)
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	return tmpFile, nil
   153→}
   154→
```

> AGENT

**Found the root cause!**

The `extractFilePathFromInput` function checks for keys `["file_path", "path", "file", "filename"]` but OpenCode's tools use `"filePath"` (camelCase). Look at the transcript:

```json
"input": {
    "filePath": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/test/opencode_test1/random_letter.rb",
    "newString": "...",
    "oldString": "..."
}
```

`"filePath"` is NOT in the lookup list, so `extractFilePathFromInput` returns `""` for ALL OpenCode tool calls. The transcript-based file extraction has been silently broken.

**Why the first turn worked**: The file was NEW (untracked), detected by `DetectFileChanges` via `changes.New`. Transcript extraction was empty but didn't matter.

**Why the resumed turn failed**: The file was now tracked/modified. `changes.Modified` IS populated by `DetectFileChanges` but **never used** in `handleLifecycleTurnEnd` (only `changes.New` and `changes.Deleted` are used). And transcript extraction returned empty due to the key mismatch.

Let me also check if `changes.Modified` is intentionally unused.

```json
  "-C": 2
}
```

> AGENT

Here's the full picture of the bug:

**Root cause**: `extractFilePathFromInput()` at `transcript.go:164` checks for keys `["file_path", "path", "file", "filename"]` but OpenCode tools use `"filePath"` (camelCase). This means transcript-based file extraction returns empty for ALL OpenCode tool calls.

**Why first turn worked**: `random_letter.rb` was a new untracked file → detected by `DetectFileChanges().New`  
**Why resumed turn failed**: `random_letter.rb` was now tracked + modified → `DetectFileChanges().Modified` is populated but never used in `handleLifecycleTurnEnd`, and transcript extraction returned empty due to key mismatch.

Let me fix it.
